package com.studentplanner.app;

import android.Manifest;
import android.app.Activity;

public class MainActivity extends Activity {
    // Subscription product id you create in Google Play Console (Monetize > Subscriptions).
    static final String SKU = "pro_monthly";
    // SHA-256 (hex, no colons) of your signing certificate. Empty = skip the check.
    static final String EXPECTED_SIG = "";
    static final String HOST = "appassets.androidplatform.net";

    private WebView web;
    private ValueCallback<Uri[]> picker;
    private boolean picking = false;     // true while a picker is open, so the app lock does not trigger
    private long leftAt = 0;
    private String toneKind = "rem";
    private final ExecutorService ex = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor();
    private final AtomicInteger importing = new AtomicInteger();
    private BillingClient bc;
    private ProductDetails details;
    private volatile boolean pro = false;
    private volatile String priceText = "\u20B910 / month";

    @Override
    protected void onCreate(Bundle b) {
        super.onCreate(b);
        if (!sigOk()) { finish(); return; }
        getWindow().setFlags(WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_SECURE, WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_SECURE);
        ReminderReceiver.channelFor(this, "info");
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 33
            && checkSelfPermission(Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS) != PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED) {
            requestPermissions(new String[]{Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS}, 1);
        }
        web = new WebView(this);
        web.setBackgroundColor(Color.parseColor("#f4f4f6"));
        setContentView(web);
        WebSettings s = web.getSettings();
        s.setJavaScriptEnabled(true);
        s.setDomStorageEnabled(true);
        s.setAllowFileAccess(false);
        s.setAllowContentAccess(false);
        s.setSupportZoom(false);
        s.setBuiltInZoomControls(false);
        s.setDisplayZoomControls(false);
        s.setTextZoom(100);
        s.setMediaPlaybackRequiresUserGesture(false);
        web.setOverScrollMode(WebView.OVER_SCROLL_NEVER);
        web.addJavascriptInterface(new Bridge(), "Android");

        final WebViewAssetLoader loader = new WebViewAssetLoader.Builder()
            .addPathHandler("/assets/", new WebViewAssetLoader.AssetsPathHandler(this)).build();
        web.setWebViewClient(new WebViewClient() {
            @Override
            public WebResourceResponse shouldInterceptRequest(WebView v, WebResourceRequest r) {
                return loader.shouldInterceptRequest(r.getUrl());
            }
            @Override
            public boolean shouldOverrideUrlLoading(WebView v, WebResourceRequest r) {
                return !HOST.equals(r.getUrl().getHost());
            }
        });
        web.setWebChromeClient(new WebChromeClient() {
            @Override
            public boolean onShowFileChooser(WebView v, ValueCallback<Uri[]> cb, FileChooserParams p) {
                if (picker != null) picker.onReceiveValue(null);
                picker = cb;
                try {
                    picking = true;
                    startActivityForResult(p.createIntent(), 7);
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    picking = false;
                    picker = null;
                    return false;
                }
                return true;
            }
        });
        web.loadUrl("https://" + HOST + "/assets/index.html");
        initBilling();
    }

    // ---- pickers ----
    @Override
    protected void onActivityResult(int rq, int rs, Intent d) {
        super.onActivityResult(rq, rs, d);
        picking = false;
        if (rq == 7 && picker != null) {
            picker.onReceiveValue(WebChromeClient.FileChooserParams.parseResult(rs, d));
            picker = null;
        } else if (rq == 8 && rs == RESULT_OK && d != null) {
            saveTone(d);
        } else if (rq == 9 && rs == RESULT_OK && d != null) {
            final ArrayList<Uri> us = new ArrayList<>();
            ClipData cd = d.getClipData();
            if (cd != null) {
                for (int i = 0; i < cd.getItemCount(); i++) us.add(cd.getItemAt(i).getUri());
            } else if (d.getData() != null) {
                us.add(d.getData());
            }
            importPdfs(us);
        } else if (rq == 10 && rs == RESULT_OK && d != null && d.getData() != null) {
            final Uri u = d.getData();
            toast("Restoring your data\u2026");
            ex.execute(() -> restore(u));
        }
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("deprecation")
    private void saveTone(Intent d) {
        Uri u = d.getParcelableExtra(RingtoneManager.EXTRA_RINGTONE_PICKED_URI);
        SharedPreferences.Editor e = getSharedPreferences("tone", MODE_PRIVATE).edit();
        if (u == null) {
            e.remove(toneKind + "_uri").remove(toneKind + "_name");
        } else {
            String name = "Custom sound";
            try {
                Ringtone r = RingtoneManager.getRingtone(this, u);
                if (r != null) name = r.getTitle(this);
            } catch (Exception ignored) { }
            e.putString(toneKind + "_uri", u.toString()).putString(toneKind + "_name", name);
        }
        e.apply();
        ReminderReceiver.channelFor(this, toneKind);
        web.evaluateJavascript("window.onTone&&onTone()", null);
    }

    @Override
    public void onRequestPermissionsResult(int rc, String[] p, int[] r) {
        super.onRequestPermissionsResult(rc, p, r);
        if (rc == 1 && r.length > 0 && r[0] == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED) {
            SharedPreferences sp = getSharedPreferences("r", MODE_PRIVATE);
            String t = sp.getString("wt", null);
            if (t != null) {
                ReminderReceiver.show(this, 7001, t, sp.getString("wb", ""), "info", false);
                sp.edit().remove("wt").remove("wb").apply();
            }
        }
    }

    // ---- lifecycle: app lock and flushing ----
    @Override
    protected void onResume() {
        super.onResume();
        if (leftAt > 0) {
            long ms = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime() - leftAt;
            leftAt = 0;
            web.evaluateJavascript("window.onAway&&onAway(" + ms + ")", null);
        }
        if (bc != null && bc.isReady()) queryPurchases();
    }

    @Override
    protected void onPause() {
        if (web != null) web.evaluateJavascript("window.__flush&&__flush()", null);
        super.onPause();
    }

    @Override
    protected void onStop() {
        if (!picking) leftAt = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime();
        super.onStop();
    }

    @Override
    @SuppressWarnings("deprecation")
    public void onBackPressed() {
        web.evaluateJavascript("(window.onBack&&onBack())?1:0", v -> {
            if (!"1".equals(v)) finish();
        });
    }

    private void toast(final String m) {
        runOnUiThread(() -> Toast.makeText(this, m, Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show());
    }

    private void welcome(final String t, final String b) {
        NotificationManager nm = (NotificationManager) getSystemService(NOTIFICATION_SERVICE);
        if (nm.areNotificationsEnabled()) {
            ReminderReceiver.show(this, 7001, t, b, "info", false);
        } else {
            getSharedPreferences("r", MODE_PRIVATE).edit().putString("wt", t).putString("wb", b).apply();
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 33) {
                runOnUiThread(() -> requestPermissions(new String[]{Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS}, 1));
            }
        }
    }

    // ---- file helpers ----
    private File dir(String n) {
        File d = new File(getFilesDir(), n);
        if (!d.exists()) d.mkdirs();
        return d;
    }

    private static boolean safe(String s) {
        return s != null && s.matches("[A-Za-z0-9_\\-]{1,80}");
    }

    private static String readFile(File f) throws IOException {
        return new String(Files.readAllBytes(f.toPath()), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
    }

    private static void writeFile(File f, String s) throws IOException {
        Files.write(f.toPath(), s.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
    }

    private static void deleteRec(File f) {
        File[] c = f.listFiles();
        if (c != null) for (File x : c) deleteRec(x);
        f.delete();
    }

    private void js(final String code) {
        runOnUiThread(() -> { if (web != null) web.evaluateJavascript(code, null); });
    }

    // ---- PDFs: copy into the app, read the text, OCR the scanned pages ----
    private void importPdfs(final List<Uri> us) {
        if (us.isEmpty()) return;
        toast("Adding " + us.size() + (us.size() == 1 ? " PDF\u2026" : " PDFs\u2026"));
        importing.incrementAndGet();
        ex.execute(() -> {
            try {
                for (Uri u : us) importOne(u);
            } finally {
                importing.decrementAndGet();
                js("window.onPdf&&onPdf()");
            }
        });
    }

    private String displayName(Uri u) {
        String n = "document.pdf";
        Cursor c = null;
        try {
            c = getContentResolver().query(u, null, null, null, null);
            if (c != null && c.moveToFirst()) {
                int i = c.getColumnIndex(OpenableColumns.DISPLAY_NAME);
                if (i >= 0 && c.getString(i) != null) n = c.getString(i);
            }
        } catch (Exception ignored) {
        } finally {
            if (c != null) c.close();
        }
        return n;
    }

    private void writeMeta(String id, String name, int pages, int done, long size, String state, long ts) {
        try {
            JSONObject o = new JSONObject();
            o.put("id", id);
            o.put("name", name);
            o.put("pages", pages);
            o.put("done", done);
            o.put("size", size);
            o.put("state", state);
            o.put("ts", ts);
            writeFile(new File(dir("pdfs"), id + ".json"), o.toString());
        } catch (Exception ignored) { }
    }

    private void importOne(Uri u) {
        String id = Long.toString(System.currentTimeMillis(), 36) + Integer.toString(new Random().nextInt(1296), 36);
        String name = displayName(u).replaceAll("(?i)\\.pdf$", "");
        File f = new File(dir("pdfs"), id + ".pdf");
        long ts = System.currentTimeMillis();
        try (InputStream in = getContentResolver().openInputStream(u); OutputStream out = new FileOutputStream(f)) {
            byte[] buf = new byte[65536];
            int n;
            while ((n = in.read(buf)) > 0) out.write(buf, 0, n);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            f.delete();
            return;
        }
        writeMeta(id, name, 0, 0, f.length(), "reading", ts);
        js("window.onPdf&&onPdf()");
        extract(id, name, f, ts);
    }

    private void extract(String id, String name, File f, long ts) {
        try {
            PDFBoxResourceLoader.init(getApplicationContext());
            PDDocument doc = PDDocument.load(f);
            int n = doc.getNumberOfPages();
            String[] pages = new String[n];
            PDFTextStripper st = new PDFTextStripper();
            for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
                st.setStartPage(i + 1);
                st.setEndPage(i + 1);
                String t;
                try { t = st.getText(doc); } catch (Exception e) { t = ""; }
                pages[i] = t == null ? "" : t.replaceAll("[ \\t]+", " ").trim();
                if (i % 4 == 0 || i == n - 1) {
                    writeMeta(id, name, n, i + 1, f.length(), "reading", ts);
                    js("window.onPdf&&onPdf()");
                }
            }
            doc.close();
            ocrMissing(id, name, f, ts, pages);
            JSONArray arr = new JSONArray();
            for (String p : pages) arr.put(p);
            writeFile(new File(dir("pdfs"), id + ".txt"), arr.toString());
            writeMeta(id, name, n, n, f.length(), "ready", ts);
        } catch (Throwable e) {
            writeMeta(id, name, 0, 0, f.length(), "failed", ts);
        }
        js("window.onPdf&&onPdf()");
    }

    // Pages with no real text (scans) are read with on-device text recognition.
    private void ocrMissing(String id, String name, File f, long ts, String[] pages) {
        ArrayList<Integer> need = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int i = 0; i < pages.length; i++) if (pages[i].length() < 8) need.add(i);
        if (need.isEmpty()) return;
        PdfRenderer r = null;
        ParcelFileDescriptor pfd = null;
        TextRecognizer rec = null;
        try {
            pfd = ParcelFileDescriptor.open(f, ParcelFileDescriptor.MODE_READ_ONLY);
            r = new PdfRenderer(pfd);
            rec = TextRecognition.getClient(TextRecognizerOptions.DEFAULT_OPTIONS);
            int k = 0;
            for (int i : need) {
                PdfRenderer.Page pg = r.openPage(i);
                int w = 1500;
                int h = Math.max(1, Math.round(1500f * pg.getHeight() / pg.getWidth()));
                Bitmap bmp = Bitmap.createBitmap(w, h, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888);
                bmp.eraseColor(Color.WHITE);
                pg.render(bmp, null, null, PdfRenderer.Page.RENDER_MODE_FOR_DISPLAY);
                pg.close();
                try {
                    pages[i] = Tasks.await(rec.process(InputImage.fromBitmap(bmp, 0))).getText().trim();
                } catch (Exception ignored) { }
                bmp.recycle();
                k++;
                if (k % 3 == 0) {
                    writeMeta(id, name, pages.length, pages.length - need.size() + k, f.length(), "reading", ts);
                    js("window.onPdf&&onPdf()");
                }
            }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
        } finally {
            try { if (rec != null) rec.close(); } catch (Throwable ignored) { }
            try { if (r != null) r.close(); } catch (Throwable ignored) { }
            try { if (pfd != null) pfd.close(); } catch (Throwable ignored) { }
        }
    }

    // ---- backup and restore ----
    private void zipDir(ZipOutputStream z, File d, String prefix) throws IOException {
        File[] fs = d.listFiles();
        if (fs == null) return;
        byte[] buf = new byte[65536];
        for (File f : fs) {
            if (!f.isFile()) continue;
            z.putNextEntry(new ZipEntry(prefix + f.getName()));
            try (InputStream in = new FileInputStream(f)) {
                int n;
                while ((n = in.read(buf)) > 0) z.write(buf, 0, n);
            }
            z.closeEntry();
        }
    }

    private void backup(final String ls) {
        ex.execute(() -> {
            String name = "Sapp-backup-" + new SimpleDateFormat("yyyyMMdd-HHmm", Locale.US).format(new Date()) + ".zip";
            try {
                ContentValues v = new ContentValues();
                v.put(MediaStore.Downloads.DISPLAY_NAME, name);
                v.put(MediaStore.Downloads.MIME_TYPE, "application/zip");
                v.put(MediaStore.Downloads.RELATIVE_PATH, Environment.DIRECTORY_DOWNLOADS);
                Uri u = getContentResolver().insert(MediaStore.Downloads.EXTERNAL_CONTENT_URI, v);
                try (ZipOutputStream z = new ZipOutputStream(new BufferedOutputStream(getContentResolver().openOutputStream(u)))) {
                    z.setLevel(1);
                    z.putNextEntry(new ZipEntry("ls.json"));
                    z.write(ls.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
                    z.closeEntry();
                    zipDir(z, dir("pics"), "pics/");
                    zipDir(z, dir("pdfs"), "pdfs/");
                }
                js("window.onBackup&&onBackup({\"ok\":true,\"name\":" + JSONObject.quote(name) + "})");
            } catch (Exception e) {
                js("window.onBackup&&onBackup({\"ok\":false})");
            }
        });
    }

    private void restore(Uri u) {
        File tmp = new File(getFilesDir(), "restore_tmp");
        try {
            deleteRec(tmp);
            tmp.mkdirs();
            String ls = null;
            String root = tmp.getCanonicalPath() + File.separator;
            try (ZipInputStream z = new ZipInputStream(new BufferedInputStream(getContentResolver().openInputStream(u)))) {
                ZipEntry e;
                byte[] buf = new byte[65536];
                while ((e = z.getNextEntry()) != null) {
                    if (e.isDirectory()) continue;
                    String n = e.getName();
                    if (n.equals("ls.json")) {
                        ByteArrayOutputStream bo = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
                        int c;
                        while ((c = z.read(buf)) > 0) bo.write(buf, 0, c);
                        ls = new String(bo.toByteArray(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
                    } else if (n.startsWith("pics/") || n.startsWith("pdfs/")) {
                        File out = new File(tmp, n);
                        if (!out.getCanonicalPath().startsWith(root)) continue;
                        out.getParentFile().mkdirs();
                        try (OutputStream os = new FileOutputStream(out)) {
                            int c;
                            while ((c = z.read(buf)) > 0) os.write(buf, 0, c);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            if (ls == null) throw new IOException("not a backup");
            deleteRec(dir("pics"));
            deleteRec(dir("pdfs"));
            File p1 = new File(tmp, "pics");
            File p2 = new File(tmp, "pdfs");
            if (p1.exists()) p1.renameTo(new File(getFilesDir(), "pics"));
            if (p2.exists()) p2.renameTo(new File(getFilesDir(), "pdfs"));
            deleteRec(tmp);
            js("window.onRestore&&onRestore(" + JSONObject.quote(ls) + ")");
        } catch (Exception e) {
            deleteRec(tmp);
            js("window.onRestore&&onRestore(null)");
        }
    }

    // ---- Google Play Billing ----
    private void initBilling() {
        bc = BillingClient.newBuilder(this)
            .setListener((r, list) -> {
                if (r.getResponseCode() == BillingClient.BillingResponseCode.OK && list != null) handle(list);
            })
            .enablePendingPurchases(PendingPurchasesParams.newBuilder().enableOneTimeProducts().build())
            .build();
        bc.startConnection(new BillingClientStateListener() {
            @Override public void onBillingSetupFinished(BillingResult r) {
                if (r.getResponseCode() == BillingClient.BillingResponseCode.OK) {
                    queryPurchases();
                    queryDetails();
                }
            }
            @Override public void onBillingServiceDisconnected() { }
        });
    }

    private void queryPurchases() {
        bc.queryPurchasesAsync(
            QueryPurchasesParams.newBuilder().setProductType(BillingClient.ProductType.SUBS).build(),
            (r, list) -> { if (r.getResponseCode() == BillingClient.BillingResponseCode.OK) handle(list); });
    }

    private void queryDetails() {
        QueryProductDetailsParams q = QueryProductDetailsParams.newBuilder()
            .setProductList(Collections.singletonList(
                QueryProductDetailsParams.Product.newBuilder()
                    .setProductId(SKU).setProductType(BillingClient.ProductType.SUBS).build()))
            .build();
        bc.queryProductDetailsAsync(q, (r, list) -> {
            if (r.getResponseCode() == BillingClient.BillingResponseCode.OK && list != null && !list.isEmpty()) {
                details = list.get(0);
                List<ProductDetails.SubscriptionOfferDetails> o = details.getSubscriptionOfferDetails();
                if (o != null && !o.isEmpty()) {
                    priceText = o.get(0).getPricingPhases().getPricingPhaseList().get(0).getFormattedPrice() + " / month";
                }
            }
        });
    }

    private void handle(List<Purchase> list) {
        boolean p = false;
        for (Purchase pu : list) {
            if (pu.getPurchaseState() == Purchase.PurchaseState.PURCHASED && pu.getProducts().contains(SKU)) {
                p = true;
                if (!pu.isAcknowledged()) {
                    bc.acknowledgePurchase(
                        AcknowledgePurchaseParams.newBuilder().setPurchaseToken(pu.getPurchaseToken()).build(), r -> { });
                }
            }
        }
        pro = p;
        js("window.onPro&&onPro()");
    }

    private void subscribe() {
        runOnUiThread(() -> {
            if (bc == null || details == null) return;
            List<ProductDetails.SubscriptionOfferDetails> o = details.getSubscriptionOfferDetails();
            if (o == null || o.isEmpty()) return;
            BillingFlowParams.ProductDetailsParams pd = BillingFlowParams.ProductDetailsParams.newBuilder()
                .setProductDetails(details).setOfferToken(o.get(0).getOfferToken()).build();
            bc.launchBillingFlow(this, BillingFlowParams.newBuilder()
                .setProductDetailsParamsList(Collections.singletonList(pd)).build());
        });
    }

    // ---- tamper check ----
    @SuppressWarnings("deprecation")
    private boolean sigOk() {
        if (EXPECTED_SIG.isEmpty()) return true;
        try {
            Signature[] sigs;
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 28) {
                sigs = getPackageManager().getPackageInfo(getPackageName(), PackageManager.GET_SIGNING_CERTIFICATES)
                    .signingInfo.getApkContentsSigners();
            } else {
                sigs = getPackageManager().getPackageInfo(getPackageName(), PackageManager.GET_SIGNATURES).signatures;
            }
            MessageDigest md = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256");
            for (Signature g : sigs) {
                StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
                for (byte x : md.digest(g.toByteArray())) sb.append(String.format("%02x", x));
                if (sb.toString().equalsIgnoreCase(EXPECTED_SIG)) return true;
            }
        } catch (Exception ignored) { }
        return false;
    }

    // ---- JS bridge ----
    class Bridge {
        // Debug builds are unlocked so you can test; release builds follow Google Play only.
        @JavascriptInterface public boolean isPro() { return BuildConfig.DEBUG || pro; }
        @JavascriptInterface public String price() { return priceText; }
        @JavascriptInterface public void subscribe() { MainActivity.this.subscribe(); }
        @JavascriptInterface public void refresh() {
            runOnUiThread(() -> { if (bc != null && bc.isReady()) queryPurchases(); });
        }

        // reminders, timer, welcome
        @JavascriptInterface public void sync(String json) { ReminderReceiver.schedule(MainActivity.this, json); }
        @JavascriptInterface public void timerStart(double end, String label) {
            ReminderReceiver.timer(MainActivity.this, (long) end, label);
        }
        @JavascriptInterface public void timerCancel() { ReminderReceiver.cancelTimer(MainActivity.this); }
        @JavascriptInterface public void welcome(String t, String b) { MainActivity.this.welcome(t, b); }

        // tones
        @JavascriptInterface public String tones() {
            return "{\"timer\":" + JSONObject.quote(ReminderReceiver.toneName(MainActivity.this, "timer"))
                + ",\"rem\":" + JSONObject.quote(ReminderReceiver.toneName(MainActivity.this, "rem")) + "}";
        }
        @JavascriptInterface public void pickTone(final String kind) {
            runOnUiThread(() -> {
                try {
                    toneKind = "timer".equals(kind) ? "timer" : "rem";
                    Intent i = new Intent(RingtoneManager.ACTION_RINGTONE_PICKER);
                    i.putExtra(RingtoneManager.EXTRA_RINGTONE_TYPE, RingtoneManager.TYPE_ALL);
                    i.putExtra(RingtoneManager.EXTRA_RINGTONE_SHOW_DEFAULT, true);
                    i.putExtra(RingtoneManager.EXTRA_RINGTONE_SHOW_SILENT, false);
                    i.putExtra(RingtoneManager.EXTRA_RINGTONE_TITLE, "timer".equals(toneKind) ? "Timer tone" : "Notification tone");
                    String cur = ReminderReceiver.toneUri(MainActivity.this, toneKind);
                    if (cur.length() > 0) i.putExtra(RingtoneManager.EXTRA_RINGTONE_EXISTING_URI, Uri.parse(cur));
                    picking = true;
                    startActivityForResult(i, 8);
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    picking = false;
                    toast("Could not open the sound picker.");
                }
            });
        }
        @JavascriptInterface public void testTone(String kind) {
            String k = "timer".equals(kind) ? "timer" : "rem";
            ReminderReceiver.show(MainActivity.this, 7002, "Sound test", "This is how your "
                + ("timer".equals(k) ? "timer" : "notification") + " will sound.", k, false);
        }

        // status and settings screens
        @JavascriptInterface public String status() {
            NotificationManager nm = (NotificationManager) getSystemService(NOTIFICATION_SERVICE);
            AlarmManager am = (AlarmManager) getSystemService(ALARM_SERVICE);
            PowerManager pm = (PowerManager) getSystemService(POWER_SERVICE);
            boolean n = nm.areNotificationsEnabled();
            boolean ex2 = Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < 31 || am.canScheduleExactAlarms();
            boolean bat = pm.isIgnoringBatteryOptimizations(getPackageName());
            return "{\"v\":4,\"notif\":" + n + ",\"exact\":" + ex2 + ",\"battery\":" + bat + "}";
        }

        @JavascriptInterface public void open(final String what) {
            runOnUiThread(() -> {
                try {
                    Intent i;
                    String pkg = getPackageName();
                    if ("notif".equals(what)) {
                        i = new Intent(Settings.ACTION_APP_NOTIFICATION_SETTINGS).putExtra(Settings.EXTRA_APP_PACKAGE, pkg);
                    } else if ("battery".equals(what)) {
                        i = new Intent(Settings.ACTION_REQUEST_IGNORE_BATTERY_OPTIMIZATIONS, Uri.parse("package:" + pkg));
                    } else if ("exact".equals(what)) {
                        i = new Intent(Settings.ACTION_REQUEST_SCHEDULE_EXACT_ALARM, Uri.parse("package:" + pkg));
                    } else {
                        i = new Intent(Settings.ACTION_APPLICATION_DETAILS_SETTINGS, Uri.parse("package:" + pkg));
                    }
                    i.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK);
                    startActivity(i);
                } catch (Exception ignored) { }
            });
        }

        // Matches the status bar and navigation bar to the app theme.
        @JavascriptInterface @SuppressWarnings("deprecation")
        public void setBars(final String hex, final boolean dark) {
            runOnUiThread(() -> {
                try {
                    int c = Color.parseColor(hex);
                    getWindow().setStatusBarColor(c);
                    getWindow().setNavigationBarColor(c);
                    web.setBackgroundColor(c);
                    if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 30) {
                        WindowInsetsController wc = getWindow().getInsetsController();
                        if (wc != null) {
                            int m = WindowInsetsController.APPEARANCE_LIGHT_STATUS_BARS
                                | WindowInsetsController.APPEARANCE_LIGHT_NAVIGATION_BARS;
                            wc.setSystemBarsAppearance(dark ? 0 : m, m);
                        }
                    }
                } catch (Exception ignored) { }
            });
        }

        // Saves an .ics file to Downloads and opens it in the calendar app.
        @JavascriptInterface public void saveIcs(String name, String text) {
            Uri u = null;
            try {
                ContentValues v = new ContentValues();
                v.put(MediaStore.Downloads.DISPLAY_NAME, name);
                v.put(MediaStore.Downloads.MIME_TYPE, "text/calendar");
                v.put(MediaStore.Downloads.RELATIVE_PATH, Environment.DIRECTORY_DOWNLOADS);
                u = getContentResolver().insert(MediaStore.Downloads.EXTERNAL_CONTENT_URI, v);
                OutputStream o = getContentResolver().openOutputStream(u);
                o.write(text.getBytes("UTF-8"));
                o.close();
            } catch (Exception e) {
                toast("Could not save the calendar file.");
                return;
            }
            try {
                Intent i = new Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW);
                i.setDataAndType(u, "text/calendar");
                i.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION | Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK);
                startActivity(i);
            } catch (Exception e) {
                toast("Saved to your Downloads folder. Open it to add the events to your calendar.");
            }
        }

        // photos live as files inside the app (private storage), not as links to the gallery
        @JavascriptInterface public boolean picPut(String k, String v) {
            try {
                if (!safe(k) || v == null) return false;
                writeFile(new File(dir("pics"), k + ".txt"), v);
                return true;
            } catch (Throwable e) { return false; }
        }
        @JavascriptInterface public String picGet(String k) {
            try {
                if (!safe(k)) return "";
                File f = new File(dir("pics"), k + ".txt");
                return f.exists() ? readFile(f) : "";
            } catch (Throwable e) { return ""; }
        }
        @JavascriptInterface public void picDel(String k) {
            if (safe(k)) new File(dir("pics"), k + ".txt").delete();
        }
        @JavascriptInterface public String picKeys() {
            JSONArray a = new JSONArray();
            File[] fs = dir("pics").listFiles();
            if (fs != null) {
                for (File f : fs) {
                    String n = f.getName();
                    if (n.endsWith(".txt")) a.put(n.substring(0, n.length() - 4));
                }
            }
            return a.toString();
        }

        // PDF library
        @JavascriptInterface public void pdfPick() {
            runOnUiThread(() -> {
                try {
                    Intent i = new Intent(Intent.ACTION_OPEN_DOCUMENT);
                    i.addCategory(Intent.CATEGORY_OPENABLE);
                    i.setType("application/pdf");
                    i.putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_ALLOW_MULTIPLE, true);
                    picking = true;
                    startActivityForResult(i, 9);
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    picking = false;
                    toast("Could not open the file picker.");
                }
            });
        }
        @JavascriptInterface public String pdfList() {
            try {
                ArrayList<JSONObject> l = new ArrayList<>();
                File[] fs = dir("pdfs").listFiles();
                if (fs != null) {
                    for (File f : fs) {
                        if (!f.getName().endsWith(".json")) continue;
                        JSONObject o = new JSONObject(readFile(f));
                        if ("reading".equals(o.optString("state")) && importing.get() == 0) o.put("state", "failed");
                        l.add(o);
                    }
                }
                Collections.sort(l, (x, y) -> Long.compare(y.optLong("ts"), x.optLong("ts")));
                JSONArray a = new JSONArray();
                for (JSONObject o : l) a.put(o);
                return a.toString();
            } catch (Exception e) { return "[]"; }
        }
        @JavascriptInterface public String pdfTexts(String id) {
            try {
                if (!safe(id)) return "[]";
                File f = new File(dir("pdfs"), id + ".txt");
                return f.exists() ? readFile(f) : "[]";
            } catch (Throwable e) { return "[]"; }
        }
        @JavascriptInterface public String pdfPage(String id, int page, int width) {
            PdfRenderer r = null;
            ParcelFileDescriptor pfd = null;
            try {
                if (!safe(id)) return "";
                pfd = ParcelFileDescriptor.open(new File(dir("pdfs"), id + ".pdf"), ParcelFileDescriptor.MODE_READ_ONLY);
                r = new PdfRenderer(pfd);
                int p = Math.max(0, Math.min(page, r.getPageCount() - 1));
                PdfRenderer.Page pg = r.openPage(p);
                int w = Math.max(200, Math.min(width, 3200));
                int h = Math.max(1, Math.round((float) w * pg.getHeight() / pg.getWidth()));
                Bitmap bmp = Bitmap.createBitmap(w, h, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888);
                bmp.eraseColor(Color.WHITE);
                pg.render(bmp, null, null, PdfRenderer.Page.RENDER_MODE_FOR_DISPLAY);
                pg.close();
                ByteArrayOutputStream bo = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
                bmp.compress(Bitmap.CompressFormat.JPEG, 88, bo);
                bmp.recycle();
                return "data:image/jpeg;base64," + Base64.encodeToString(bo.toByteArray(), Base64.NO_WRAP);
            } catch (Throwable e) {
                return "";
            } finally {
                try { if (r != null) r.close(); } catch (Throwable ignored) { }
                try { if (pfd != null) pfd.close(); } catch (Throwable ignored) { }
            }
        }
        @JavascriptInterface public void pdfDel(String id) {
            if (!safe(id)) return;
            new File(dir("pdfs"), id + ".pdf").delete();
            new File(dir("pdfs"), id + ".json").delete();
            new File(dir("pdfs"), id + ".txt").delete();
        }

        // download data / add existing data
        @JavascriptInterface public void exportData(String lsJson) {
            toast("Saving your data\u2026");
            backup(lsJson);
        }
        @JavascriptInterface public void importData() {
            runOnUiThread(() -> {
                try {
                    Intent i = new Intent(Intent.ACTION_OPEN_DOCUMENT);
                    i.addCategory(Intent.CATEGORY_OPENABLE);
                    i.setType("*/*");
                    picking = true;
                    startActivityForResult(i, 10);
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    picking = false;
                    toast("Could not open the file picker.");
                }
            });
        }
    }
}
=====FILE app/src/main/java/com/studentplanner/app/ReminderReceiver.java=====
package com.studentplanner.app;

import android.app.AlarmManager;
import android.app.Notification;
import android.app.NotificationChannel;
import android.app.NotificationManager;
import android.app.PendingIntent;
import android.content.BroadcastReceiver;
import android.content.Context;
import android.content.Intent;
import android.content.SharedPreferences;
import android.media.AudioAttributes;
import android.media.RingtoneManager;
import android.net.Uri;
import android.os.Build;

import org.json.JSONArray;
import org.json.JSONObject;

public class ReminderReceiver extends BroadcastReceiver {
    static final int TIMER_ID = 9001;

    // ---- tones the user picked (kind is "timer" or "rem") ----
    static String toneUri(Context c, String kind) {
        return c.getSharedPreferences("tone", Context.MODE_PRIVATE).getString(kind + "_uri", "");
    }

    static String toneName(Context c, String kind) {
        return c.getSharedPreferences("tone", Context.MODE_PRIVATE).getString(kind + "_name", "Default alarm sound");
    }

    static Uri toneOf(Context c, String kind) {
        String u = toneUri(c, kind);
        if (u.length() > 0) return Uri.parse(u);
        return RingtoneManager.getDefaultUri(RingtoneManager.TYPE_ALARM);
    }

    // A notification channel keeps its sound for life, so each tone gets its own channel.
    static String channelFor(Context c, String kind) {
        NotificationManager nm = (NotificationManager) c.getSystemService(Context.NOTIFICATION_SERVICE);
        if ("info".equals(kind)) {
            nm.createNotificationChannel(new NotificationChannel("info4", "Welcome", NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_DEFAULT));
            return "info4";
        }
        Uri tone = toneOf(c, kind);
        String id = kind + "_" + Integer.toHexString(String.valueOf(tone).hashCode());
        if (nm.getNotificationChannel(id) == null) {
            for (NotificationChannel old : nm.getNotificationChannels()) {
                if (old.getId().startsWith(kind + "_")) nm.deleteNotificationChannel(old.getId());
            }
            AudioAttributes aa = new AudioAttributes.Builder()
                .setUsage(AudioAttributes.USAGE_ALARM)
                .setContentType(AudioAttributes.CONTENT_TYPE_SONIFICATION).build();
            NotificationChannel ch = new NotificationChannel(id, "timer".equals(kind) ? "Timer" : "Reminders",
                NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_HIGH);
            if (tone != null) ch.setSound(tone, aa);
            ch.enableVibration(true);
            ch.setVibrationPattern(new long[]{0, 500, 250, 500, 250, 500});
            nm.createNotificationChannel(ch);
        }
        return id;
    }

    static void show(Context c, int id, String title, String body, String kind, boolean insist) {
        if (body == null) body = "";
        NotificationManager nm = (NotificationManager) c.getSystemService(Context.NOTIFICATION_SERVICE);
        Notification.Builder b = new Notification.Builder(c, channelFor(c, kind));
        PendingIntent open = PendingIntent.getActivity(c, 0, new Intent(c, MainActivity.class),
            PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE | PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT);
        b.setSmallIcon(R.drawable.ic_stat)
         .setColor(0xFF3B6CF0)
         .setContentTitle(title)
         .setContentText(body)
         .setStyle(new Notification.BigTextStyle().bigText(body))
         .setAutoCancel(true)
         .setContentIntent(open);
        boolean alarm = !"info".equals(kind);
        if (alarm) {
            b.setCategory(Notification.CATEGORY_ALARM);
            b.setTimeoutAfter(90000);
        }
        Notification n = b.build();
        if (alarm && insist) n.flags |= Notification.FLAG_INSISTENT;   // keep beeping until you tap it
        nm.notify(id, n);
    }

    static PendingIntent pi(Context c, int id, String kind, String t, String b) {
        Intent i = new Intent(c, ReminderReceiver.class);
        i.putExtra("k", kind);
        i.putExtra("t", t);
        i.putExtra("b", b);
        i.putExtra("i", id);
        return PendingIntent.getBroadcast(c, id, i, PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT | PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE);
    }

    static void arm(Context c, long at, PendingIntent p) {
        AlarmManager am = (AlarmManager) c.getSystemService(Context.ALARM_SERVICE);
        try {
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 31 && !am.canScheduleExactAlarms()) {
                am.setAndAllowWhileIdle(AlarmManager.RTC_WAKEUP, at, p);
            } else {
                am.setExactAndAllowWhileIdle(AlarmManager.RTC_WAKEUP, at, p);
            }
        } catch (SecurityException e) {
            am.setAndAllowWhileIdle(AlarmManager.RTC_WAKEUP, at, p);
        }
    }

    static void timer(Context c, long end, String label) {
        c.getSharedPreferences("r", Context.MODE_PRIVATE).edit().putLong("tm_end", end).putString("tm_label", label).apply();
        arm(c, end, pi(c, TIMER_ID, "timer", label + " done", "Time is up. Take a short break."));
    }

    static void cancelTimer(Context c) {
        c.getSharedPreferences("r", Context.MODE_PRIVATE).edit().remove("tm_end").apply();
        AlarmManager am = (AlarmManager) c.getSystemService(Context.ALARM_SERVICE);
        am.cancel(pi(c, TIMER_ID, "timer", "", ""));
        ((NotificationManager) c.getSystemService(Context.NOTIFICATION_SERVICE)).cancel(TIMER_ID);
    }

    static void schedule(Context c, String json) {
        try {
            AlarmManager am = (AlarmManager) c.getSystemService(Context.ALARM_SERVICE);
            SharedPreferences sp = c.getSharedPreferences("r", Context.MODE_PRIVATE);
            int old = sp.getInt("n", 0);
            for (int i = 0; i < old; i++) am.cancel(pi(c, i, "rem", "", ""));
            JSONArray a = new JSONArray(json);
            int n = Math.min(a.length(), 60);
            long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
            for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
                JSONObject o = a.getJSONObject(i);
                long at = o.getLong("at");
                if (at > now) arm(c, at, pi(c, i, "rem", o.getString("t"), o.optString("b", "")));
            }
            sp.edit().putInt("n", n).putString("json", json).apply();
        } catch (Exception ignored) { }
    }

    @Override
    public void onReceive(Context c, Intent i) {
        String k = i.getStringExtra("k");
        show(c, i.getIntExtra("i", 0), i.getStringExtra("t"), i.getStringExtra("b"), k == null ? "rem" : k, true);
    }

    public static class Boot extends BroadcastReceiver {
        @Override
        public void onReceive(Context c, Intent i) {
            SharedPreferences sp = c.getSharedPreferences("r", Context.MODE_PRIVATE);
            schedule(c, sp.getString("json", "[]"));
            long end = sp.getLong("tm_end", 0);
            if (end > System.currentTimeMillis()) timer(c, end, sp.getString("tm_label", "Focus session"));
        }
    }
}

<script>
(function(){
var KEY='student-planner-v2',OLD='attendance-tracker-v1',LK='student-planner-lock';
var DEMO=false;
var S=load(),tab='home',pend=null,sheet=null,evd='',locked=hasLock(),cryptoKey=null,saltB=null,fails=0,hiddenAt=0,draft=[],toasts=[],smp=null,imgOK=false,scanMsg='',sa=false,an=true,WKEY='student-planner-wall',wallData='';
try{wallData=localStorage.getItem(WKEY)||''}catch(e){}
if(!pro())tab='notes';
var $=function(id){return document.getElementById(id)};
function uid(){return Date.now().toString(36)+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,6)}
function hasLock(){try{return !!localStorage.getItem(LK)}catch(e){return false}}
function norm(d){
  d=d&&typeof d==='object'?d:{};
  d.subjects=d.subjects||[];d.notes=d.notes||[];d.projects=d.projects||[];d.events=d.events||[];
  d.name=d.name||'';d.theme=d.theme||'system';d.wall=d.wall||'none';d.scratch=d.scratch||'';d.remind=d.remind||'17:00';
  d.focusLen=d.focusLen||25;d.focus=d.focus||{date:'',min:0,n:0};d.target=Math.min(90,Math.max(50,d.target||75));
  d.calcH=d.calcH||[];d.profile=d.profile||{};d.profileDone=!!d.profileDone;d.welcomed=!!d.welcomed;d.tasks=d.tasks||[];d.photos=d.photos||[];d.walls=d.walls||[];
  d.gpa=d.gpa||{rows:[{n:'',c:'3',g:'O'}],sems:[]};d.grades=d.grades||{'O':10,'A+':9,'A':8,'B+':7,'B':6,'C':5,'P':4,'F':0};d.tm=d.tm||null;
  if(!d.subjects.some(function(x){return x.id===d.sel}))d.sel=d.subjects[0]?d.subjects[0].id:null;
  return d}
function load(){
  if(hasLock())return norm({});
  var d=null;try{d=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY))}catch(e){}
  if(!d){d={};try{var o=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(OLD));if(Array.isArray(o))d.subjects=o}catch(e){}}
  return norm(d)}
var svT=null;
function flush(){svT=null;if(locked)return;if(cryptoKey)saveEnc();else{try{localStorage.setItem(KEY,JSON.stringify(S))}catch(e){}}syncNative()}
function save(){if(locked)return;clearTimeout(svT);svT=setTimeout(flush,300)}
var te=new TextEncoder(),td=new TextDecoder();
function b64(buf){var u=new Uint8Array(buf),t='',i;for(i=0;i<u.length;i+=8192)t+=String.fromCharCode.apply(null,u.subarray(i,i+8192));return btoa(t)}
function unb64(t){var r=atob(t),u=new Uint8Array(r.length),i;for(i=0;i<r.length;i++)u[i]=r.charCodeAt(i);return u}
function derive(pin,salt){
  return crypto.subtle.importKey('raw',te.encode(pin),'PBKDF2',false,['deriveKey']).then(function(km){
    return crypto.subtle.deriveKey({name:'PBKDF2',salt:salt,iterations:250000,hash:'SHA-256'},km,{name:'AES-GCM',length:256},false,['encrypt','decrypt'])})}
function saveEnc(){
  if(!cryptoKey||locked)return Promise.resolve(false);
  var iv=crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(12));
  return crypto.subtle.encrypt({name:'AES-GCM',iv:iv},cryptoKey,te.encode(JSON.stringify({S:S,w:wallData}))).then(function(ct){
    try{localStorage.setItem(LK,JSON.stringify({s:b64(saltB),i:b64(iv),c:b64(ct)}));return true}catch(e){return false}}).catch(function(){return false})}
function openRec(pin){
  var rec=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(LK)),salt=unb64(rec.s);
  return derive(pin,salt).then(function(k){
    return crypto.subtle.decrypt({name:'AES-GCM',iv:unb64(rec.i)},k,unb64(rec.c)).then(function(pt){return{k:k,salt:salt,o:JSON.parse(td.decode(pt))}})})}
var _db=null;
function idb(){return _db||(_db=new Promise(function(res,rej){var r=indexedDB.open('planner',1);r.onupgradeneeded=function(){r.result.createObjectStore('f')};r.onsuccess=function(){res(r.result)};r.onerror=function(){rej(r.error)}}))}
function idbOp(mode,fn){return idb().then(function(db){return new Promise(function(res,rej){var tx=db.transaction('f',mode),rq=fn(tx.objectStore('f'));tx.oncomplete=function(){res(rq&&rq.result)};tx.onerror=tx.onabort=function(){rej(tx.error)}})})}
function idbGet(k){return idbOp('readonly',function(st){return st.get(k)})}
function idbSet(k,v){return idbOp('readwrite',function(st){return st.put(v,k)})}
function idbDel(k){return idbOp('readwrite',function(st){return st.delete(k)})}
function idbKeys(){return idbOp('readonly',function(st){return st.getAllKeys()})}
function packV(str){
  if(!cryptoKey)return Promise.resolve(str);
  var iv=crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(12));
  return crypto.subtle.encrypt({name:'AES-GCM',iv:iv},cryptoKey,te.encode(str)).then(function(ct){return{iv:iv,ct:ct}})}
function unpackV(v){
  if(v==null)return Promise.resolve('');
  if(typeof v==='string')return Promise.resolve(v);
  if(!cryptoKey)return Promise.resolve('');
  return crypto.subtle.decrypt({name:'AES-GCM',iv:v.iv},cryptoKey,v.ct).then(function(b){return td.decode(b)}).catch(function(){return ''})}
function eStr(v){return typeof v==='string'?v:'E'+JSON.stringify({iv:Array.prototype.slice.call(v.iv),ct:b64(v.ct)})}
function eVal(t){if(!t)return null;if(t.charAt(0)!=='E')return t;try{var o=JSON.parse(t.slice(1));return{iv:new Uint8Array(o.iv),ct:unb64(o.ct).buffer}}catch(e){return null}}
var NATP=!!(window.Android&&Android.picPut);
function lsPic(k){var t=null;try{t=localStorage.getItem('pic:'+k)}catch(e){}return t?unpackV(eVal(t)):Promise.resolve('')}
function putPic(k,str){return packV(str).then(function(v){
  if(NATP){if(!Android.picPut(k,eStr(v)))throw new Error('the app storage is full');return}
  return idbSet(k,v).then(function(){try{localStorage.removeItem('pic:'+k)}catch(e){}},function(){
    try{localStorage.setItem('pic:'+k,eStr(v))}catch(e){throw new Error('the phone storage is full')}})})}
function getPic(k){
  if(NATP){var t=Android.picGet(k);return t?unpackV(eVal(t)):Promise.resolve('')}
  return idbGet(k).then(function(v){return v!=null?unpackV(v):lsPic(k)},function(){return lsPic(k)})}
function delPic(k){
  if(NATP){try{Android.picDel(k)}catch(e){}return Promise.resolve()}
  try{localStorage.removeItem('pic:'+k)}catch(e){}return idbDel(k).catch(function(){})}
function picKeys(){
  if(NATP){try{return Promise.resolve(JSON.parse(Android.picKeys()))}catch(e){return Promise.resolve([])}}
  return idbKeys().catch(function(){return[]}).then(function(ks){ks=ks||[];try{for(var i=0;i<localStorage.length;i++){var q=localStorage.key(i);if(q&&q.indexOf('pic:')===0&&ks.indexOf(q.slice(4))<0)ks.push(q.slice(4))}}catch(e){}return ks})}
function migratePics(fin){
  return picKeys().then(function(ks){return Promise.all(ks.map(function(k){return getPic(k).catch(function(){return ''})})).then(function(vals){
    cryptoKey=fin;
    return Promise.all(ks.map(function(k,i){return vals[i]?putPic(k,vals[i]).catch(function(){}):0}))})})}
function esc(s){return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g,function(c){return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]})}
function pc(p,a){var t=p+a;return t?p/t*100:null}
function f(n){return String(Number(n.toFixed(2)))}
function cur(){return S.subjects.find(function(x){return x.id===S.sel})}
function col(v){return v===null?'var(--mut)':v>=75?'var(--g)':'var(--r)'}
function cl(n){return n+' class'+(n===1?'':'es')}

function adv(p,a){
  var t=p+a,T=S.target;
  if(!t)return{ok:null,text:'Log a lecture to see your status'};
  if(p*100>=T*t)return{ok:true,text:'On track. Keep attending regularly.'};
  var y=Math.ceil((T*t-100*p)/(100-T));
  return{ok:false,text:'You must attend the next '+cl(y)+' consecutively to reach '+T+'%'}}

function gauge(v,T,name){
  var th=Math.PI*(1-T/100),c=Math.cos(th),s=Math.sin(th),cx=140,cy=118,arc='M50 118A90 90 0 0 1 230 118',cc=col(v);
  var n=name.length>24?name.slice(0,23)+'…':name,an=c>0.35?'start':c<-0.35?'end':'middle';
  var sw='fill="none" stroke-width="16" stroke-linecap="round" pathLength="100"';
  return '<svg viewBox="0 -8 280 160" width="100%" role="img" aria-label="Attendance gauge">'+
  '<path d="'+arc+'" '+sw+' style="stroke:var(--track)"/>'+
  (v===null?'':'<path class="garc" d="'+arc+'" '+sw+' style="stroke:'+cc+'" stroke-dasharray="'+v+' 100"/>')+
  '<line x1="'+(cx+82*c)+'" y1="'+(cy-82*s)+'" x2="'+(cx+100*c)+'" y2="'+(cy-100*s)+'" stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round" style="stroke:var(--ink)"/>'+
  '<text x="'+(cx+110*c)+'" y="'+(cy-110*s+4)+'" text-anchor="'+an+'" font-size="11" font-weight="600" style="fill:var(--mut)">'+T+'%</text>'+
  '<text x="140" y="100" text-anchor="middle" font-size="40" font-weight="700" style="fill:'+cc+'">'+(v===null?'–':f(v)+'%')+'</text>'+
  '<text x="140" y="124" text-anchor="middle" font-size="13" style="fill:var(--mut)">'+esc(n)+'</text>'+
  '<text x="50" y="148" text-anchor="middle" font-size="11" style="fill:var(--mut)">0%</text>'+
  '<text x="230" y="148" text-anchor="middle" font-size="11" style="fill:var(--mut)">100%</text></svg>'}

function home(){
  var fo=foc(),ev=upcoming().slice(0,3),h=new Date().getHours(),full=S.focusLen*60;
  var g=h<12?'Good morning':h<17?'Good afternoon':'Good evening';
  var d=dlabel(new Date());
  var sub=tmr.done?'Session done. Tap to start':tmr.run?'Tap to pause':tmr.left<full?'Paused. Tap to resume':'Tap to start';
  var o='<div class="top"><div><p class="sub">'+g+'</p><h1>'+esc(S.name||'there')+'!</h1></div>'+
  '<div class="row" style="gap:10px"><button class="gear" data-a="dark" aria-label="Toggle dark mode">'+(isDark()?SUN:MOON)+'</button><button class="gear" data-a="opensheet" data-t="settings" aria-label="Settings"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M4 7h9M17 7h3M4 17h3M11 17h9"/><circle cx="15" cy="7" r="2"/><circle cx="9" cy="17" r="2"/></svg></button></div></div>'+
  '<div class="home"><div class="card today"><div><div class="t">Today</div><div class="sub">'+d+'</div></div>'+
  '<div class="evs">'+(ev.length?ev.map(function(e){return '<div class="ev"><span class="when">'+when(e)+'</span><span class="nm">'+esc(e.title)+'</span></div>'}).join(''):'<span class="sub">No upcoming events</span>')+'</div>'+
  '<button class="lnk" data-a="opensheet" data-t="events">+ Add event</button></div>'+
  '<div class="card small"><button class="tbtn" data-a="timer"><span>Focus timer</span><b class="big" id="tmv">'+mmss(tmr.left)+'</b><span>'+sub+'</span></button>'+
  (!tmr.run&&tmr.left<full?'<button class="lnk" data-a="treset">Reset</button>':'')+'</div>'+
  '<div class="card small qn"><span>Quick note</span><textarea id="qn" rows="3" placeholder="Type here…" aria-label="Quick note">'+esc(S.scratch)+'</textarea></div></div>'+
  '<div class="row sp sec"><h2>Projects</h2><button class="lnk" data-a="tab" data-t="proj">+ Add project</button></div><div class="grid">'+
  (S.projects.length?S.projects.slice(0,3).map(function(p){
    var dn=p.subs.filter(function(x){return x.done}).length,t=p.subs.length,pr=t?Math.round(dn/t*100):0;
    return '<button class="card nrow" data-a="tab" data-t="proj"><div class="row sp"><b>'+esc(p.name||'Untitled')+'</b><span class="sub">'+pr+'%</span></div>'+
    '<div class="bar"><i style="width:'+pr+'%;background:var(--ink)"></i></div><div class="due" style="margin-top:8px;'+(over(p)?'color:var(--r)':'')+'">'+dueTxt(p)+'</div></button>'}).join('')
  :'<div class="card sub empty">No projects yet.</div>')+'</div>'+
  '<div class="row sp sec"><h2>Attendance</h2><button class="lnk" data-a="tab" data-t="att">+ Add subject</button></div><div class="grid">'+
  (S.subjects.length?S.subjects.map(function(s){
    var v=pc(s.present,s.absent),c=col(v);
    return '<div class="card row prow"><button class="pmain" data-a="open" data-id="'+s.id+'"><div class="row sp"><b>'+esc(s.name)+'</b><b style="color:'+c+'">'+(v===null?'–':f(v)+'%')+'</b></div>'+
    '<div class="bar"><i style="width:'+(v||0)+'%;background:'+c+'"></i></div></button>'+
    '<button class="circ sm gr" data-a="qp" data-id="'+s.id+'" data-k="present" aria-label="Mark '+esc(s.name)+' present">✓</button>'+
    '<button class="circ sm rd" data-a="qp" data-id="'+s.id+'" data-k="absent" aria-label="Mark '+esc(s.name)+' absent">✕</button></div>'}).join('')
  :'<div class="card sub empty">Add your first subject in the Attendance tab.</div>')+'</div>';
  return o}

function att(){
  var s=cur(),T=S.target,o='<h1>Attendance</h1>';
  if(s){
    var v=pc(s.present,s.absent),ad=adv(s.present,s.absent),t=s.present+s.absent;
    var c=col(v);
    o+='<div class="card gauge">'+gauge(v,T,s.name)+'<p class="advice" style="color:'+c+'">'+ad.text+'</p></div>'+
    '<div class="card stats"><div><span>Attended</span><b>'+s.present+'</b></div><div><span>Missed</span><b>'+s.absent+'</b></div><div><span>Total classes</span><b>'+t+'</b></div></div>'+
    '<label class="lbl" for="sel">Select subject</label><select id="sel">'+
    S.subjects.map(function(x){return '<option value="'+x.id+'"'+(x.id===s.id?' selected':'')+'>'+esc(x.name)+'</option>'}).join('')+'</select>'}
  o+='<label class="lbl" for="nm">Add new subject</label><div class="row"><input id="nm" autocapitalize="words" placeholder="e.g. Data Structures" maxlength="40" autocomplete="off"><button class="circ add" data-a="addsub" aria-label="Add subject">+</button></div>';
  if(!s)return o+'<div class="card sub empty">Add a subject to start tracking.</div>';
  function mk(k,title,word,cls){
    return '<div class="card row sp mark"><div><b>'+title+'</b><div class="sub">'+s[k]+' '+word+'</div></div><div class="row">'+
    '<button class="circ" data-a="dec" data-k="'+k+'" aria-label="Remove one">−</button>'+
    '<button class="circ '+cls+'" data-a="inc" data-k="'+k+'" aria-label="Add one">+</button></div></div>'}
  o+=mk('present','Mark Present','attended','gr')+mk('absent','Mark Absent','missed','rd')+
  '<label class="lbl" for="tg">Target attendance requirement</label><div class="row"><input type="range" id="tg" min="50" max="90" step="1" value="'+T+'"><b id="tgv" style="min-width:48px;text-align:right">'+T+'%</b></div>'+
  '<div style="text-align:center"><button class="lnk danger" data-a="delsub">'+(pend==='s'+s.id?'Tap again to delete '+esc(s.name):'Delete this subject')+'</button></div>';
  return o}

function notes(){
  var o='<h1>Notes</h1><div class="row"><input id="nt" autocapitalize="words" placeholder="New note title" maxlength="60" autocomplete="off"><button class="circ add" data-a="addnote" aria-label="Add note">+</button></div><button class="chip" data-a="opensheet" data-t="syl" style="justify-self:start">Make notes from a syllabus</button>';
  if(!S.notes.length)o+='<div class="card sub empty">Create a note, then break it into sub-notes you can tick off.</div>';
  S.notes.forEach(function(n){
    var dn=n.subs.filter(function(x){return x.done}).length,id=n.id;
    o+='<div class="card note"><input class="ntitle" data-f="title" data-id="'+id+'" value="'+esc(n.title)+'" placeholder="Title">'+
    '<textarea data-f="body" data-id="'+id+'" rows="3" placeholder="Write your notes here…">'+esc(n.body)+'</textarea>'+
    (n.subs.length?'<div class="subs">'+n.subs.map(function(x){
      return '<div class="sn'+(x.done?' dn':'')+'"><button class="chk" data-a="tog" data-id="'+id+'" data-s="'+x.id+'" aria-label="Toggle sub-note">'+(x.done?'✓':'')+'</button><span>'+esc(x.t)+(x.d?'<small class="dtl">'+esc(x.d)+'</small>':'')+'</span>'+
      '<button class="x" data-a="rmsn" data-id="'+id+'" data-s="'+x.id+'" aria-label="Remove sub-note">×</button></div>'}).join('')+'</div>':'')+
    '<div class="row"><input class="sin" data-id="'+id+'" placeholder="Add a sub-note" maxlength="80" autocomplete="off"><button class="circ sm add" data-a="addsn" data-id="'+id+'" aria-label="Add sub-note">+</button></div>'+
    '<div class="row sp"><span class="sub">'+(n.subs.length?dn+' of '+n.subs.length+' done':'No sub-notes yet')+'</span>'+
    '<button class="lnk danger" data-a="delnote" data-id="'+id+'">'+(pend==='n'+id?'Tap again to delete':'Delete note')+'</button></div></div>'});
  return o}

function ymd(d){var m=d.getMonth()+1,x=d.getDate();return d.getFullYear()+'-'+(m<10?'0':'')+m+'-'+(x<10?'0':'')+x}
function hm(d){return (d.getHours()<10?'0':'')+d.getHours()+':'+(d.getMinutes()<10?'0':'')+d.getMinutes()}
function upcoming(){
  var now=ymd(new Date())+' '+hm(new Date());
  return S.events.filter(function(e){return e.date+' '+(e.time||'23:59')>=now})
    .sort(function(a,b){return (a.date+(a.time||'00:00')).localeCompare(b.date+(b.time||'00:00'))})}
function when(e){
  var n=new Date();n.setDate(n.getDate()+1);
  var day=e.date===ymd(new Date())?'Today':e.date===ymd(n)?'Tomorrow':new Date(e.date+'T00:00').toLocaleDateString(undefined,{weekday:'short'})+' '+dmy(e.date);
  var ts=e.time?new Date('2000-01-01T'+e.time).toLocaleTimeString(undefined,{hour:'numeric',minute:'2-digit'}):'';
  return day+(ts?' · '+ts:'')}
function foc(){var t=ymd(new Date());if(S.focus.date!==t)S.focus={date:t,min:0,n:0};return S.focus}
function dur(m){return m>=60?Math.floor(m/60)+'h '+(m%60)+'m':m+' min'}
function mmss(x){var m=Math.floor(x/60),y=x%60;return (m<10?'0':'')+m+':'+(y<10?'0':'')+y}
var tmr={run:false,done:false,left:S.focusLen*60,end:0,iv:null};
function saveTm(){S.tm={run:tmr.run,end:tmr.end,left:tmr.left};clearTimeout(svT);flush()}
function tick(){
  var l=Math.ceil((tmr.end-Date.now())/1000);
  if(l<=0){finish();return}
  tmr.left=l;var e=$('tmv');if(e)e.textContent=mmss(l)}
function finish(){
  clearInterval(tmr.iv);tmr.run=false;tmr.done=true;tmr.left=S.focusLen*60;
  var fo=foc();fo.min+=S.focusLen;fo.n++;S.tm=null;clearTimeout(svT);flush();
  try{if(window.Android&&Android.timerCancel)Android.timerCancel()}catch(e){}
  if(!(window.Android&&Android.timerStart))beep();try{if(navigator.vibrate)navigator.vibrate([400,200,400,200,400])}catch(e){}
  toast('Focus session done. Time for a short break.');render()}
function restoreTimer(){
  var t=S.tm;if(!t)return;
  clearInterval(tmr.iv);
  if(t.run&&t.end>Date.now()){tmr.end=t.end;tmr.left=Math.ceil((t.end-Date.now())/1000);tmr.run=true;tmr.done=false;tmr.iv=setInterval(tick,500)}
  else if(t.run){tmr.run=false;tmr.done=true;tmr.left=S.focusLen*60;var fo=foc();fo.min+=S.focusLen;fo.n++;S.tm=null;save();toast('Your focus session finished while you were away.')}
  else if(t.left){tmr.run=false;tmr.left=t.left}}
var ac=null;
function audio(){try{if(!ac)ac=new (window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext)();if(ac.state==='suspended')ac.resume()}catch(e){}}
function beep(){
  audio();if(!ac)return;
  var t=ac.currentTime,i,o,g;
  for(i=0;i<10;i++){
    o=ac.createOscillator();g=ac.createGain();o.type='square';o.frequency.value=i%2?880:1175;
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001,t+i*0.35);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.35,t+i*0.35+0.02);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001,t+i*0.35+0.3);
    o.connect(g);g.connect(ac.destination);o.start(t+i*0.35);o.stop(t+i*0.35+0.32)}}
function ent(c,id){return (c==='p'?S.projects:S.notes).find(function(x){return x.id===id})}
function over(p){return p.due&&p.due<ymd(new Date())&&!(p.subs.length&&p.subs.every(function(x){return x.done}))}
function dueTxt(p){return p.due?'Due '+dmy(p.due):'No due date'}

function proj(){
  var o='<h1>Projects</h1><div class="row"><input id="pn" autocapitalize="words" placeholder="New project name" maxlength="50" autocomplete="off"><button class="circ add" data-a="addproj" aria-label="Add project">+</button></div>';
  if(!S.projects.length)o+='<div class="card sub empty">Add a project, set a due date, and tick off tasks to fill the progress bar.</div>';
  S.projects.forEach(function(p){
    var id=p.id,dn=p.subs.filter(function(x){return x.done}).length,t=p.subs.length,pr=t?Math.round(dn/t*100):0;
    o+='<div class="card note"><input class="ntitle" data-c="p" data-f="name" data-id="'+id+'" value="'+esc(p.name)+'" placeholder="Project name">'+
    '<div class="row"><span class="due" style="'+(over(p)?'color:var(--r)':'')+'">'+(over(p)?'Overdue:':'Due date:')+'</span>'+dmyInput('data-c="p" data-f="due" data-id="'+id+'"',p.due||'','pd')+'</div>'+
    '<div><div class="row sp"><span class="sub">'+(t?dn+' of '+t+' tasks done':'No tasks yet')+'</span><b>'+pr+'%</b></div><div class="bar"><i style="width:'+pr+'%;background:var(--ink)"></i></div></div>'+
    (t?'<div class="subs">'+p.subs.map(function(x){
      return '<div class="sn'+(x.done?' dn':'')+'"><button class="chk" data-a="tog" data-c="p" data-id="'+id+'" data-s="'+x.id+'" aria-label="Toggle task">'+(x.done?'✓':'')+'</button><span>'+esc(x.t)+'</span>'+
      '<button class="x" data-a="rmsn" data-c="p" data-id="'+id+'" data-s="'+x.id+'" aria-label="Remove task">×</button></div>'}).join('')+'</div>':'')+
    '<div class="row"><input class="sin" data-c="p" data-id="'+id+'" placeholder="Add a task" maxlength="80" autocomplete="off"><button class="circ sm add" data-a="addsn" data-c="p" data-id="'+id+'" aria-label="Add task">+</button></div>'+
    '<div style="text-align:right"><button class="lnk danger" data-a="delproj" data-id="'+id+'">'+(pend==='p'+id?'Tap again to delete':'Delete project')+'</button></div></div>'});
  return o}

var sylTxt='';
function parseSyllabus(text){
  var lines=String(text).replace(/\r/g,'').split('\n'),notes=[],cur=null,last=null,i,m;
  var BOX=/^\s*(?:[-*•]\s*)?(?:\[\s*([xX✓✔]?)\s*\]|(\(\s*[xX✓]?\s*\))|([☐□▢])|([☑☒✅✔]))\s*(.*)$/;
  var NUM=/^\s*(\d{1,2})\s*[.)]\s+(.+)$/,SUBJ=/^\s*(subject|course|paper)\s*[:\-–]\s*(.+)$/i,MD=/^\s*#{1,4}\s+(.+)$/;
  var UNIT=/^\s*(unit|module|chapter|topic|part|section)\s*[-:.]?\s*([ivx]+|\d+)\b[\s:.\-–]*(.*)$/i,BUL=/^\s*[-*•–]\s+(.*)$/;
  function nn(t){cur={id:uid(),title:t.trim().slice(0,80),body:'',subs:[]};notes.push(cur);last=null}
  function item(t,done){if(!cur)nn('Syllabus');last={id:uid(),t:t.trim().slice(0,120),d:'',done:!!done};cur.subs.push(last)}
  function nextReal(k){for(var j=k+1;j<lines.length;j++)if(lines[j].trim())return lines[j];return ''}
  function isItem(l){return BOX.test(l)||UNIT.test(l.trim())}
  for(i=0;i<lines.length;i++){
    var ln=lines[i],t=ln.trim();if(!t)continue;
    if(m=MD.exec(t)){nn(m[1]);continue}
    if(m=BOX.exec(ln)){var tx=(m[5]||'').trim();if(!tx)continue;item(tx,(m[1]&&m[1].trim())||m[4]||(m[2]&&/[xX✓]/.test(m[2])));continue}
    if(m=SUBJ.exec(t)){nn(m[2]);continue}
    if(m=NUM.exec(t)){
      var rest=m[2].trim(),nr=nextReal(i);
      if(/^[A-Za-z0-9&+\-]{1,10}\s*:/.test(rest)||isItem(nr)||!cur){nn(rest);continue}}
    if(UNIT.test(t)){item(t,false);continue}
    if(t.length<=60&&t===t.toUpperCase()&&/[A-Z]{3}/.test(t)&&isItem(nextReal(i))){nn(t);continue}
    if(m=BUL.exec(t))t=m[1].trim();
    if(last)last.d+=(last.d?'; ':'')+t;
    else{if(!cur)nn('Syllabus');cur.body+=(cur.body?'\n':'')+t}}
  return notes.filter(function(n){return n.subs.length||n.body})}
function sylPrev(){
  var r=parseSyllabus(sylTxt),n=0;r.forEach(function(x){n+=x.subs.length});
  return r.length?(r.length+(r.length===1?' subject':' subjects')+' · '+n+' checklist items found'):(sylTxt.trim()?'Nothing found yet. Use numbered headings and [ ] lines.':'')}
function sylSheet(){
  return '<h2>Make notes from a syllabus</h2><p class="sub">Paste your syllabus. Sapp finds the subject headings, units and [ ] checkboxes, and builds notes with checklists for you.</p>'+
  '<textarea id="sylt" rows="9" autocapitalize="sentences" placeholder="Paste your syllabus here">'+esc(sylTxt)+'</textarea>'+
  '<div class="sub" id="sylp">'+esc(sylPrev())+'</div><button class="go" data-a="sylgo">Create notes</button><button class="lnk" data-a="closesheet">Close</button>'}
function sheetHtml(){
  if(!sheet)return '';
  var e0=sa?' enter':'',tall=(sheet==='gpa'||sheet==='sci'||sheet==='photos'||sheet==='photo'||sheet==='tasks'||sheet==='pdfs'||sheet==='pdfv'||sheet==='syl')?' tall':'';
  var w='<div class="scrim'+e0+'" id="scrim"><div class="sheet'+e0+tall+'">',z='</div></div>';
  if(sheet==='name')return w+profForm(true)+z;
  if(sheet==='profile')return w+profForm(false)+z;
  if(sheet==='syl')return w+sylSheet()+z;
  if(sheet==='pdfs')return w+pdfSheet()+z;
  if(sheet==='pdfv')return w+pdfViewSheet()+z;
  if(sheet==='tasks')return w+tasksSheet()+z;
  if(sheet==='gpa')return w+gpaSheet()+z;
  if(sheet==='sci')return w+sciSheet()+z;
  if(sheet==='photos')return w+photosSheet()+z;
  if(sheet==='photo')return w+photoSheet()+z;
  if(sheet==='settings')return w+'<h2>Settings</h2><span class="sub">Your name</span><input id="wn" value="'+esc(S.name)+'" maxlength="30" autocomplete="off"><button class="chip" data-a="opensheet" data-t="profile">Edit profile</button>'+
    '<span class="sub">Focus session length (minutes)</span><input id="sf" type="number" inputmode="numeric" min="5" max="90" value="'+S.focusLen+'">'+
    '<span class="sub">Reminder time (sent the day before a test)</span><input id="rt" type="time" value="'+esc(S.remind)+'">'+
    statusUi()+toneUi()+themeUi()+wallUi()+lockUi()+dataUi()+'<button class="go" data-a="savename">Save</button><button class="lnk" data-a="closesheet">Close</button>'+z;
  var ev=upcoming();
  return w+'<h2>Upcoming events</h2><input id="et" autocapitalize="words" placeholder="Event title, e.g. Physics test" maxlength="60" autocomplete="off">'+
    '<div class="two">'+dmyInput('id="ed"',evd||ymd(new Date()),'')+'<input id="etm" type="time"></div>'+
    '<button class="go" data-a="addev">Add event</button>'+
    ev.map(function(e){return '<div class="evrow"><div class="ev"><span class="when">'+when(e)+'</span><span class="nm">'+esc(e.title)+'</span></div><button class="x" data-a="delev" data-id="'+e.id+'" aria-label="Delete event">×</button></div>'}).join('')+
    '<button class="lnk" data-a="closesheet">Close</button>'+z}

var THEMES=[['system','System','linear-gradient(135deg,#f4f4f6 50%,#0d0d10 50%)'],['light','Light','linear-gradient(135deg,#f4f4f6 50%,#3b6cf0 50%)'],['dark','Dark','linear-gradient(135deg,#0d0d10 50%,#3b6cf0 50%)'],['ocean','Ocean','linear-gradient(135deg,#0b1b2b 50%,#38a3ff 50%)'],['forest','Forest','linear-gradient(135deg,#0e1912 50%,#5fbf7a 50%)'],['sunset','Sunset','linear-gradient(135deg,#fff1e6 50%,#ff6a3d 50%)'],['lavender','Lavender','linear-gradient(135deg,#f2efff 50%,#7b5cf0 50%)']];
var WALLS=[['aurora','linear-gradient(135deg,#7f7fd5,#86a8e7,#91eae4)'],['dusk','linear-gradient(160deg,#2c3e50,#fd746c)'],['peach','linear-gradient(135deg,#ffecd2,#fcb69f)'],['night','linear-gradient(160deg,#0f2027,#203a43,#2c5364)'],['mint','linear-gradient(135deg,#d4fc79,#96e6a1)'],['berry','linear-gradient(135deg,#a18cd1,#fbc2eb)']];
function isDark(){
  var v=getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue('--bg').trim();
  if(v.charAt(0)!=='#')return false;
  if(v.length===4)v='#'+v[1]+v[1]+v[2]+v[2]+v[3]+v[3];
  var n=parseInt(v.slice(1),16);
  return ((n>>16)*299+((n>>8)&255)*587+(n&255)*114)/1000<128}
var wallCache={id:'',url:''},wallKey='',wallLoad='',barKey='';
function bars(){
  if(!(window.Android&&Android.setBars))return;
  var bg=getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue('--bg').trim();
  if(bg.charAt(0)!=='#')return;
  var k=bg+isDark();if(k===barKey)return;barKey=k;
  try{Android.setBars(bg,isDark())}catch(e){}}
function applyTheme(){
  var r=document.documentElement,css='',w,id;
  if(S.theme==='system')r.removeAttribute('data-skin');else r.setAttribute('data-skin',S.theme);
  if(S.wall.indexOf('p:')===0){
    id=S.wall.slice(2);
    if(wallCache.id===id)css='url('+wallCache.url+') center/cover no-repeat';
    else if(wallLoad!==id){wallLoad=id;getPic('w'+id).then(function(u){wallLoad='';if(u&&S.wall==='p:'+id){wallCache={id:id,url:u};applyTheme()}}).catch(function(){wallLoad=''})}}
  else{w=WALLS.find(function(x){return x[0]===S.wall});if(w)css=w[1]}
  if(css!==wallKey){wallKey=css;$('wall').style.background=css;r.classList.toggle('wp',!!css)}
  bars()}

function dmy(iso){var m=/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/.exec(iso||'');return m?m[3]+'/'+m[2]+'/'+m[1]:''}
function parseDmy(t){var d=String(t||'').replace(/\D/g,'');if(d.length!==8)return null;var dd=+d.slice(0,2),mm=+d.slice(2,4),yy=+d.slice(4),c=new Date(yy,mm-1,dd);if(yy<1900||c.getFullYear()!==yy||c.getMonth()!==mm-1||c.getDate()!==dd)return null;return yy+'-'+p2(mm)+'-'+p2(dd)}
function maskDmy(t){var d=t.value.replace(/\D/g,'').slice(0,8),o=d;if(d.length>4)o=d.slice(0,2)+'/'+d.slice(2,4)+'/'+d.slice(4);else if(d.length>2)o=d.slice(0,2)+'/'+d.slice(2);t.value=o}
function dmyInput(attr,iso,cls){return '<input class="'+cls+' dmy" type="text" inputmode="numeric" placeholder="dd/mm/yyyy" maxlength="10" autocomplete="off" '+attr+' value="'+dmy(iso)+'">'}
function dlabel(d){return d.toLocaleDateString(undefined,{weekday:'long'})+', '+dmy(ymd(d))}
function p2(n){return (n<10?'0':'')+n}
function trig(e){var d=new Date(e.date+'T00:00');d.setDate(d.getDate()-1);var q=(S.remind||'17:00').split(':');d.setHours(+q[0]||17,+q[1]||0,0,0);return d.getTime()}
function evStart(e){return new Date(e.date+'T'+(e.time||'23:59')).getTime()}
function remTxt(e){var d=new Date(trig(e));return dmy(ymd(d))+' '+d.toLocaleTimeString(undefined,{hour:'numeric',minute:'2-digit'})}
function soon(){var l=new Date();l.setDate(l.getDate()+2);var z=ymd(l);return upcoming().some(function(e){return e.date<=z})}
function drawToasts(){var t=$('toasts');if(t)t.innerHTML=toasts.map(function(m,i){return '<div class="toast"><span>'+esc(m)+'</span><button data-a="closetoast" data-i="'+i+'" aria-label="Dismiss">×</button></div>'}).join('')}
function toast(m){toasts.push(m);if(toasts.length>4)toasts.shift();drawToasts();setTimeout(function(){var i=toasts.indexOf(m);if(i>=0){toasts.splice(i,1);drawToasts()}},5000)}
function checkRem(){
  if(locked||!pro())return;
  var now=Date.now(),ch=false;
  S.events.forEach(function(e){
    if(e.notified||trig(e)>now||evStart(e)<now)return;
    e.notified=true;ch=true;
    toast('Coming up: '+e.title+' · '+when(e));
    try{if(window.Notification&&Notification.permission==='granted')new Notification('Upcoming: '+e.title,{body:when(e)})}catch(x){}});
  if(ch){save();if(tab==='sched'||tab==='home')render()}}
function syncNative(){
  try{
    if(!window.Android||!Android.sync||locked)return;
    var now=Date.now(),l=[];
    if(pro())S.events.forEach(function(e){var t=trig(e);if(t>now&&evStart(e)>now)l.push({t:((e.kind&&e.kind!=='Event')?e.kind:'Event')+' tomorrow: '+e.title,b:absTxt(e)+'\nReminder from Sapp',at:t})});
    Android.sync(JSON.stringify(l))}catch(x){}}
function icsEsc(t){return String(t).replace(/\\/g,'\\\\').replace(/;/g,'\\;').replace(/,/g,'\\,').replace(/\n/g,' ')}
function icsUtc(d){return d.getUTCFullYear()+p2(d.getUTCMonth()+1)+p2(d.getUTCDate())+'T'+p2(d.getUTCHours())+p2(d.getUTCMinutes())+'00Z'}
function ics(){
  var L=['BEGIN:VCALENDAR','VERSION:2.0','PRODID:-//Sapp//EN'];
  upcoming().forEach(function(e){
    var d=e.date.replace(/-/g,'');
    L.push('BEGIN:VEVENT','UID:'+e.id+'@student-planner','DTSTAMP:'+icsUtc(new Date()));
    if(e.time){
      var en=new Date(e.date+'T'+e.time);en.setHours(en.getHours()+1);
      L.push('DTSTART:'+d+'T'+e.time.replace(':','')+'00','DTEND:'+d+'T'+(ymd(en)===e.date?p2(en.getHours())+p2(en.getMinutes()):'2359')+'00')}
    else L.push('DTSTART;VALUE=DATE:'+d);
    L.push('SUMMARY:'+icsEsc(e.title),'BEGIN:VALARM','ACTION:DISPLAY','DESCRIPTION:'+icsEsc(e.title),'TRIGGER;VALUE=DATE-TIME:'+icsUtc(new Date(trig(e))),'END:VALARM','END:VEVENT')});
  L.push('END:VCALENDAR');return L.join('\r\n')}
function readImg(file){
  function viaReader(){return new Promise(function(res,rej){var rd=new FileReader();rd.onerror=function(){rej(new Error('could not read the file'))};rd.onload=function(){var im=new Image();im.onload=function(){res(im)};im.onerror=function(){rej(new Error('could not open the image'))};im.src=rd.result};rd.readAsDataURL(file)})}
  function viaBitmap(){return window.createImageBitmap?createImageBitmap(file,{resizeWidth:2400,resizeQuality:'medium'}):Promise.reject(new Error('no bitmap'))}
  function fallback(res,rej){viaBitmap().then(res,function(){viaReader().then(res,rej)})}
  return new Promise(function(res,rej){
    var u=null,im=new Image();
    try{u=URL.createObjectURL(file)}catch(e){fallback(res,rej);return}
    im.onload=function(){res(im)};
    im.onerror=function(){fallback(res,rej)};
    im.src=u})}
function draw(im,max){var k=Math.min(1,max/Math.max(im.width,im.height)),c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=Math.max(1,Math.round(im.width*k));c.height=Math.max(1,Math.round(im.height*k));c.getContext('2d').drawImage(im,0,0,c.width,c.height);return c}
function toBlobP(im,max,q){return new Promise(function(res,rej){draw(im,max).toBlob(function(b){b?res(b):rej(new Error('blob'))},'image/jpeg',q)})}
function storeWall(u){try{if(cryptoKey){wallData=u;saveEnc();return true}localStorage.setItem(WKEY,u);wallData=u;return true}catch(e){return false}}

function loadTess(){
  if(window.Tesseract)return Promise.resolve(window.Tesseract);
  return new Promise(function(res,rej){
    var sc=document.createElement('script');sc.src='https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/tesseract.js@5.1.1/dist/tesseract.min.js';
    sc.onload=function(){window.Tesseract?res(window.Tesseract):rej(new Error('tess'))};sc.onerror=function(){rej(new Error('net'))};document.head.appendChild(sc)})}
function ocrText(im){return loadTess().then(function(T){return T.recognize(draw(im,2000),'eng')}).then(function(r){return (r&&r.data&&r.data.text)||''})}
var MON={jan:0,feb:1,mar:2,apr:3,may:4,jun:5,jul:6,aug:7,sep:8,oct:9,nov:10,dec:11};
var KD={exam:'Exam',test:'Test',quiz:'Test',viva:'Exam',practical:'Exam',assignment:'Assignment',submission:'Assignment',project:'Assignment',lab:'Class'};
function mkDate(d,m,y){
  var n=new Date(),t0=new Date(n.getFullYear(),n.getMonth(),n.getDate());
  if(y===null||y===undefined){y=t0.getFullYear();if(new Date(y,m,d)<t0)y++}
  else if(y<100)y+=2000;
  var c=new Date(y,m,d);
  return(c.getMonth()!==m||c.getDate()!==d)?null:ymd(c)}
function parseTT(text){
  var out=[],MN='(jan|feb|mar|apr|may|jun|jul|aug|sep|oct|nov|dec)';
  String(text).split(/\n+/).forEach(function(raw){
    var ln=raw.replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim(),date=null,rest=ln,m;
    if(ln.length<4)return;
    if(m=ln.match(/\b(20\d\d)[-\/.](\d{1,2})[-\/.](\d{1,2})\b/)){date=mkDate(+m[3],+m[2]-1,+m[1]);rest=ln.replace(m[0],' ')}
    else if(m=ln.match(/\b(\d{1,2})[-\/.](\d{1,2})[-\/.](\d{2,4})\b/)){date=mkDate(+m[1],+m[2]-1,+m[3]);rest=ln.replace(m[0],' ')}
    else if(m=ln.match(new RegExp('\\b(\\d{1,2})(?:st|nd|rd|th)?[\\s-]+'+MN+'[a-z]*\\.?,?(?:\\s+(\\d{4}))?','i'))){date=mkDate(+m[1],MON[m[2].toLowerCase()],m[3]?+m[3]:null);rest=ln.replace(m[0],' ')}
    else if(m=ln.match(new RegExp('\\b'+MN+'[a-z]*\\.?\\s+(\\d{1,2})(?:st|nd|rd|th)?(?:,?\\s+(\\d{4}))?\\b','i'))){date=mkDate(+m[2],MON[m[1].toLowerCase()],m[3]?+m[3]:null);rest=ln.replace(m[0],' ')}
    if(!date)return;
    var tm='',hh,mm=0,ap='',t=rest.match(/\b(\d{1,2})[:.](\d{2})\s*(am|pm)?/i);
    if(t){hh=+t[1];mm=+t[2];ap=(t[3]||'').toLowerCase();rest=rest.replace(t[0],' ')}
    else if(t=rest.match(/\b(\d{1,2})\s*(am|pm)\b/i)){hh=+t[1];ap=t[2].toLowerCase();rest=rest.replace(t[0],' ')}
    if(hh!==undefined&&hh<=23&&mm<60){if(ap==='pm'&&hh<12)hh+=12;if(ap==='am'&&hh===12)hh=0;tm=p2(hh)+':'+p2(mm)}
    var kw=rest.match(/exam|test|quiz|viva|practical|assignment|submission|project|lab/i),kind=kw?KD[kw[0].toLowerCase()]:'Event';
    var title=rest.replace(/\b(mon|tue|wed|thu|fri|sat|sun)[a-z]*\b\.?/ig,' ').replace(/\s+/g,' ').replace(/^[\s|:;,\-–]+|[\s|:;,\-–]+$/g,'').replace(/\s+(on|at|by|due)$/i,'');
    out.push({title:(title.length>1?title:kind).slice(0,80),date:date,time:tm,kind:kind})});
  return out}
/*END-TT*/
function linesToItems(t){
  var seen={},out=[];
  String(t).split(/\n+/).forEach(function(l){
    l=l.replace(/\s+/g,' ').replace(/^(\d+[.)]|[-–•*·])\s*/,'').trim();
    var k=l.toLowerCase();
    if(l.replace(/[^A-Za-z]/g,'').length<3||seen[k])return;
    seen[k]=1;out.push(l)});
  return out}

function statusUi(){
  var st=nst();
