var calc={e:'',r:'',deg:true,ans:0,eq:false};
function evalX(src){
  var s=src.replace(/\s+/g,''),i=0,pct=false,multi=false;
  function fn(f,a){
    var k=calc.deg?Math.PI/180:1;
    switch(f){
      case 'sin':return Math.sin(a*k);case 'cos':return Math.cos(a*k);case 'tan':return Math.tan(a*k);
      case 'asin':return Math.asin(a)/k;case 'acos':return Math.acos(a)/k;case 'atan':return Math.atan(a)/k;
      case 'ln':return Math.log(a);case 'log':return Math.log10(a);case 'sqrt':return Math.sqrt(a);case 'abs':return Math.abs(a)}
    throw 0}
  function primary(){
    var c=s[i],m,v;
    if(c==='('){i++;v=expr();if(s[i]===')')i++;return v}
    m=/^(\d+\.?\d*|\.\d+)/.exec(s.slice(i));
    if(m){i+=m[0].length;return parseFloat(m[0])}
    m=/^[a-z]+/.exec(s.slice(i));
    if(m){
      var f=m[0];i+=f.length;
      if(f==='pi')return Math.PI;if(f==='e')return Math.E;if(f==='ans')return calc.ans;
      if(s[i]!=='(')throw 0;
      i++;v=expr();if(s[i]===')')i++;return fn(f,v)}
    throw 0}
  function post(){
    var v=primary();pct=false;
    while(s[i]==='!'||s[i]==='%'){
      if(s[i]==='!'){if(v<0||v!==Math.floor(v)||v>170)throw 0;var r=1,j;for(j=2;j<=v;j++)r*=j;v=r}else{v=v/100;pct=true}
      i++}
    return v}
  function pow(){var b=post();if(s[i]==='^'){i++;return Math.pow(b,unary())}return b}
  function unary(){if(s[i]==='-'){i++;return -unary()}if(s[i]==='+'){i++;return unary()}return pow()}
  function term(){
    var v=unary();multi=false;
    for(;;){
      var c=s[i],r;
      if(c==='*'||c==='/'){i++;r=unary();multi=true;v=c==='*'?v*r:v/r}
      else if(c==='('||(c&&/[a-z]/.test(c))){v*=unary();multi=true}
      else break}
    return v}
  function expr(){
    var v=term();
    while(s[i]==='+'||s[i]==='-'){var o=s[i++],r=term();if(pct&&!multi)r=v*r;v=o==='+'?v+r:v-r}
    return v}
  var out=expr();
  if(i<s.length||!isFinite(out))throw 0;
  return out}
function fmtN(n){return String(parseFloat(n.toPrecision(12)))}
var cal={inv:false,sci:false,hist:false,tab:'calc'};
function dsp(e){return e.replace(/\*/g,'×').replace(/\//g,'÷').replace(/pi/g,'π').replace(/sqrt\(/g,'√(').replace(/-/g,'−')}
function calcLive(){
  if(!calc.e||calc.eq||!/[-+*\/^!%(]|[a-z]/.test(calc.e.replace(/^-/,'')))return '';
  try{return fmtN(evalX(calc.e))}catch(x){return ''}}
var PAD=[['C','AC','fn'],['( )','PAR','fn'],['%','%','fn'],['÷','/','op'],['7','7',''],['8','8',''],['9','9',''],['×','*','op'],['4','4',''],['5','5',''],['6','6',''],['−','-','op'],['1','1',''],['2','2',''],['3','3',''],['+','+','op'],['+/−','NEG','fn'],['0','0',''],['.','.',''],['=','=','eq']];
function sciKeys(){var i=cal.inv;return [
  ['INV','INV','s'+(i?' on':'')],[i?'sin⁻¹':'sin',i?'asin(':'sin(','s'],[i?'cos⁻¹':'cos',i?'acos(':'cos(','s'],[i?'tan⁻¹':'tan',i?'atan(':'tan(','s'],
  [calc.deg?'DEG':'RAD','DEG','s'],['ln','ln(','s'],['log','log(','s'],['√','sqrt(','s'],
  ['x²','^2','s'],['xʸ','^','s'],['n!','!','s'],['π','pi','s'],
  ['e','e','s'],['Ans','ans','s'],['(','(','s'],[')',')','s']]}
function keyBtn(k){return '<button class="ck '+k[2]+'" data-a="ck" data-v="'+k[1]+'">'+k[0]+'</button>'}

var UN={
 Length:[['Kilometre','km',1000],['Metre','m',1],['Centimetre','cm',0.01],['Millimetre','mm',0.001],['Mile','mi',1609.344],['Yard','yd',0.9144],['Foot','ft',0.3048],['Inch','in',0.0254]],
 Area:[['Square kilometre','km²',1e6],['Square metre','m²',1],['Hectare','ha',1e4],['Acre','ac',4046.8564224],['Square foot','ft²',0.09290304],['Square inch','in²',0.00064516]],
 Volume:[['Litre','L',1],['Millilitre','mL',0.001],['Cubic metre','m³',1000],['Gallon (US)','gal',3.785411784],['Quart (US)','qt',0.946352946],['Cup (US)','cup',0.2365882365],['Fluid ounce (US)','fl oz',0.0295735295625]],
 Mass:[['Tonne','t',1000],['Kilogram','kg',1],['Gram','g',0.001],['Milligram','mg',1e-6],['Pound','lb',0.45359237],['Ounce','oz',0.028349523125]],
 Temperature:[['Celsius','°C',1],['Fahrenheit','°F',1],['Kelvin','K',1]],

 Data:[['Byte','B',1],['Kilobyte','KB',1e3],['Megabyte','MB',1e6],['Gigabyte','GB',1e9],['Terabyte','TB',1e12],['Bit','bit',0.125]],
 Pressure:[['Pascal','Pa',1],['Kilopascal','kPa',1000],['Bar','bar',1e5],['Atmosphere','atm',101325],['PSI','psi',6894.757293168],['mmHg','mmHg',133.322387415]],
 Energy:[['Joule','J',1],['Kilojoule','kJ',1000],['Calorie','cal',4.184],['Kilocalorie','kcal',4184],['Watt-hour','Wh',3600],['Kilowatt-hour','kWh',3.6e6]],
 Power:[['Watt','W',1],['Kilowatt','kW',1000],['Horsepower','hp',745.69987158227]],

 Currency:null};
var FXC=['USD','INR','EUR','GBP','JPY','AUD','CAD','AED','SGD','CNY','CHF','SAR'],FX={r:null,t:0},fxMsg='';

function unitsOf(c){return c==='Currency'?FXC.map(function(k){return[k,k,(FX.r&&FX.r[k])?1/FX.r[k]:0]}):UN[c]}
function convert(c,v,a,b){
  if(c==='Temperature'){var k=a===0?v:a===1?(v-32)*5/9:v-273.15;return b===0?k:b===1?k*9/5+32:k+273.15}
  var U=unitsOf(c);if(!U[a][2]||!U[b][2])return NaN;return v*U[a][2]/U[b][2]}
function cvOut(){var n=parseFloat(String(cv.v).replace(',','.'));if(isNaN(n))return '—';var r=convert(cv.cat,n,cv.a,cv.b);return isNaN(r)?'—':String(parseFloat(r.toPrecision(10)))}
function cvSet(t){var k=t.dataset.cv;cv[k]=k==='v'?t.value:+t.value;var o=$('cvo');if(o)o.textContent=cvOut()}
function loadFx(){
  try{var c=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('sapp-fx'));if(c&&c.r)FX=c}catch(e){}
  if(FX.r&&Date.now()-FX.t<6*3600000)return;
  fxMsg='Loading live rates…';
  fetch('https://open.er-api.com/v6/latest/USD').then(function(r){return r.json()}).then(function(j){
    if(!j||!j.rates)throw 0;FX={r:j.rates,t:Date.now()};fxMsg='';try{localStorage.setItem('sapp-fx',JSON.stringify(FX))}catch(e){}
  }).catch(function(){fxMsg=FX.r?'Using saved rates (offline).':'Live rates need internet.'}).then(function(){if(sheet==='sci'&&cal.tab==='conv'&&cv.cat==='Currency')rs()})}

var pdfs=[],pdfTx={},pq='',pres=[],pqT=null,pvw={id:'',page:1,zoom:1,mode:'page'};
function pdfRefresh(){try{pdfs=JSON.parse(Android.pdfList())}catch(e){pdfs=[]}}
function pdfLoad(id){
  if(pdfTx[id])return pdfTx[id];
  var a=[];try{a=JSON.parse(Android.pdfTexts(id))}catch(e){}
  return pdfTx[id]={raw:a,low:a.map(function(t){return String(t).toLowerCase()})}}
function qToks(q){var o=[],re=/"([^"]+)"|(\S+)/g,m;while(m=re.exec(q))o.push((m[1]||m[2]).toLowerCase());return o}
function pdfSearch(q){
  var ws=qToks(q),out=[];if(!ws.length)return out;
  pdfs.forEach(function(d){
    if(d.state!=='ready')return;
    var t=pdfLoad(d.id),i,j,L,ok,sc,c,n,pp,f,a;
    for(i=0;i<t.low.length;i++){
      L=t.low[i];ok=true;sc=0;
      for(j=0;j<ws.length;j++){
        c=L.indexOf(ws[j]);if(c<0){ok=false;break}
        sc+=1;n=0;pp=c;while(pp>=0&&n<30){n++;pp=L.indexOf(ws[j],pp+ws[j].length)}sc+=n}
      if(ok){f=L.indexOf(ws[0]);a=Math.max(0,f-70);
        out.push({id:d.id,name:d.name,page:i+1,sc:sc,sn:(a>0?'…':'')+String(t.raw[i]).slice(a,f+ws[0].length+110).replace(/\s+/g,' ')})}}});
  out.sort(function(x,y){return y.sc-x.sc});return out.slice(0,120)}
function hlTxt(t,toks){
  if(!toks.length)return esc(t);
  var re=new RegExp('('+toks.map(function(x){return x.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g,'\\$&')}).join('|')+')','gi');
  return String(t).split(re).map(function(p,i){return i%2?'<mark>'+esc(p)+'</mark>':esc(p)}).join('')}
function mb(n){return (n/1048576).toFixed(1)+' MB'}
function pdfResHtml(){
  if(!pq.trim())return '';
  if(!pres.length)return '<div class="card sub empty">No match. Try fewer or different words.</div>';
  var toks=qToks(pq);
  return '<div class="sub">'+pres.length+(pres.length>=120?'+':'')+' matches</div>'+pres.map(function(r){
    return '<button class="hrow" data-a="pdfopen" data-id="'+r.id+'" data-p="'+r.page+'" data-m="text"><span class="sub">'+esc(r.name)+' · page '+r.page+'</span><span>'+hlTxt(r.sn,toks)+'</span></button>'}).join('')}
function pdfListHtml(){
  if(!pdfs.length)return '<div class="card sub empty">No PDFs yet. Tap Add PDFs.</div>';
  return pdfs.map(function(d){
    var st=d.state==='ready'?d.pages+' pages · '+mb(d.size):d.state==='reading'?'Reading page '+d.done+' of '+(d.pages||'?')+'…':'Could not read this file';
    return '<div class="evrow"><div class="ev"><span class="nm">'+esc(d.name)+'</span><span class="when">'+st+'</span></div>'+
    (d.state==='ready'?'<button class="lnk" data-a="pdfopen" data-id="'+d.id+'" data-p="1">Open</button>':'')+
    '<button class="lnk danger" data-a="pdfdel" data-id="'+d.id+'">'+(pend==='pd'+d.id?'Sure?':'Delete')+'</button></div>'}).join('')}
function runSearch(){var r=$('pres');if(!r)return;pres=pq.trim()?pdfSearch(pq):[];r.innerHTML=pdfResHtml()}
function pdfInit(){pdfRefresh();var l=$('pdfl');if(l)l.innerHTML=pdfListHtml();runSearch()}

  '<button class="go" data-a="pdfpick">Add PDFs</button>'+
  '<input id="pq" type="search" placeholder="Search words in all PDFs" autocomplete="off" enterkeyhint="search" value="'+esc(pq)+'">'+
  '<div id="pres" class="grid"></div><div id="pdfl" class="grid"></div><button class="lnk" data-a="closesheet">Close</button>'}
function pdfViewSheet(){
  var d=pdfs.find(function(x){return x.id===pvw.id});
  if(!d)return '<h2>PDF</h2><button class="lnk" data-a="opensheet" data-t="pdfs">Back</button>';
  return '<div class="row sp"><b class="vname">'+esc(d.name)+'</b><button class="lnk" data-a="opensheet" data-t="pdfs">Back</button></div>'+
  '<div class="seg"><button class="'+(pvw.mode==='page'?'on':'')+'" data-a="pvmode" data-v="page">Page</button><button class="'+(pvw.mode==='text'?'on':'')+'" data-a="pvmode" data-v="text">Text</button></div>'+
  '<div class="row sp"><button class="chip" data-a="pvgo" data-v="-1">◀</button><span class="row"><input id="pvp" class="pgn" type="text" inputmode="numeric" value="'+pvw.page+'"><span class="sub">of '+d.pages+'</span></span><button class="chip" data-a="pvgo" data-v="1">▶</button></div>'+
  (pvw.mode==='page'?'<div class="row sp"><button class="chip" data-a="pvz" data-v="-1">−</button><span class="sub">Zoom '+Math.round(pvw.zoom*100)+'%</span><button class="chip" data-a="pvz" data-v="1">+</button></div><div class="pvbox" id="pvbox"><img id="pvimg" alt=""></div>':'<div class="pvtext" id="pvtext"></div>')+
  '<button class="chip" data-a="pdfnotes" data-id="'+d.id+'" style="justify-self:start">Make notes from this PDF</button>'}
function pvLoad(){
  var d=pdfs.find(function(x){return x.id===pvw.id});if(!d)return;
  if(pvw.mode==='text'){
    var t=pdfLoad(d.id).raw[pvw.page-1],el=$('pvtext');
    if(el)el.innerHTML=hlTxt(t||'(No text on this page.)',qToks(pq)).replace(/\n/g,'<br>');return}
  var im=$('pvimg'),box=$('pvbox');if(!im)return;
  var w=Math.min(3200,Math.round(((box&&box.clientWidth)||340)*(window.devicePixelRatio||2)*pvw.zoom));
  im.style.width=(pvw.zoom*100)+'%';
  setTimeout(function(){try{im.src=Android.pdfPage(d.id,pvw.page-1,w)}catch(e){}},20)}

var lastSheet='';
function rs(){
  var wv=$('wn'),sv=$('sf'),rv=$('rt'),sc=document.querySelector('.sheet'),st=(sc&&lastSheet===sheet)?sc.scrollTop:0;
  var wval=wv?wv.value:null,sfval=sv?sv.value:null,rtval=rv?rv.value:null;
  $('sheet').innerHTML=sheetHtml();sa=false;lastSheet=sheet;
  if(wval!==null&&$('wn'))$('wn').value=wval;
  if(sfval!==null&&$('sf'))$('sf').value=sfval;
  if(rtval!==null&&$('rt'))$('rt').value=rtval;
  sc=document.querySelector('.sheet');if(sc)sc.scrollTop=st;
  applyTheme();
  if(sheet==='photos')loadThumbs();
  if(sheet==='photo')loadPv();
  if(sheet==='pdfs')pdfInit();
  if(sheet==='pdfv')pvLoad()}

function render(){
  document.body.classList.toggle('lk',locked);
  if(locked){$('app').innerHTML='';$('sheet').innerHTML=lockHtml();applyTheme();return}
  var nm=$('nm'),keep=nm?nm.value:'';
  $('app').innerHTML=(!pro()&&tab!=='notes')?paywall():tab==='home'?home():tab==='tools'?toolsPage():tab==='att'?att():tab==='proj'?proj():tab==='sched'?sched():notes();
  $('app').classList.toggle('anim',an);an=false;
  rs();
  if(keep&&$('nm'))$('nm').value=keep;
  document.querySelectorAll('nav button').forEach(function(b){b.classList.toggle('on',b.dataset.t===tab);if(b.dataset.t==='sched')b.classList.toggle('hasdot',pro()&&soon())})}

function bump(id,k,d){
  var s=S.subjects.find(function(x){return x.id===id});if(!s)return;
  s[k]=Math.max(0,s[k]+d);save();render()}
function note(id){return S.notes.find(function(x){return x.id===id})}
function confirmTwice(key,fn){
  if(pend!==key){pend=key;render();setTimeout(function(){if(pend===key){pend=null;render()}},3500);return}
  pend=null;fn()}

  ck:function(b){
    var v=b.dataset.v,r,e;
    if(v==='AC'){calc.e='';calc.r='';calc.eq=false}
    else if(v==='SCI')cal.sci=!cal.sci;
    else if(v==='HIST')cal.hist=!cal.hist;
    else if(v==='INV')cal.inv=!cal.inv;
    else if(v==='DEG')calc.deg=!calc.deg;
    else if(v==='DEL'){calc.eq=false;calc.r='';calc.e=calc.e.replace(/(asin\(|acos\(|atan\(|sin\(|cos\(|tan\(|ln\(|log\(|sqrt\(|ans|pi|.)$/,'')}
    else if(v==='PAR'){
      if(calc.eq){calc.e='';calc.r='';calc.eq=false}
      var o=(calc.e.match(/\(/g)||[]).length,c=(calc.e.match(/\)/g)||[]).length;
      calc.e+=(o>c&&/[\d)!%]$|pi$|e$|ans$/.test(calc.e))?')':'('}
    else if(v==='NEG'){
      if(calc.eq){calc.e='ans';calc.eq=false;calc.r=''}
      e=calc.e;
      if(/\(-(\d+\.?\d*)\)$/.test(e))calc.e=e.replace(/\(-(\d+\.?\d*)\)$/,'$1');
      else if(/(\d+\.?\d*)$/.test(e))calc.e=e.replace(/(\d+\.?\d*)$/,'(-$1)');
      else calc.e=e+'(-'}
    else if(v==='='){if(calc.e){try{r=evalX(calc.e);calc.ans=r;calc.r=fmtN(r)}catch(x){calc.r='Error'}calc.eq=true;if(calc.r!=='Error'){S.calcH.unshift({e:calc.e,r:calc.r});if(S.calcH.length>100)S.calcH.length=100;save()}}}
    else{
      if(calc.eq){calc.e=(/^[-+*\/^!%]$/.test(v)&&calc.r!=='Error')?'ans':'';calc.r='';calc.eq=false}
      calc.e+=v}
    rs()},

  unlock:function(){
    var pn=$('pin'),m=$('pmsg');if(!pn||!pn.value)return;
    openRec(pn.value).then(function(r){
      S=norm(r.o.S);wallData=r.o.w||'';cryptoKey=r.k;saltB=r.salt;locked=false;fails=0;

    }).catch(function(){
      fails++;pn.value='';
      if(m)m.textContent=fails>=5?'Too many tries. Wait 30 seconds.':'Wrong PIN.';
      if(fails>=5){pn.disabled=true;setTimeout(function(){fails=0;pn.disabled=false;if(m)m.textContent=''},30000)}})},
  lockNow:function(){
    saveEnc().then(function(){

  setpin:function(){
    var a=$('p1').value,b=$('p2').value,m=$('pmsg');
    if(!window.crypto||!crypto.subtle){m.textContent='Encryption is not available here.';return}
    if(a.length<4){m.textContent='Use at least 4 characters.';return}
    if(a!==b){m.textContent='The two PINs do not match.';return}
    m.style.color='var(--mut)';m.textContent='Turning on the lock…';
    saltB=crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(16));
    derive(a,saltB).then(function(k){return migratePics(k)}).then(function(){return saveEnc()}).then(function(ok){
      if(!ok)throw new Error('could not store the data');
      try{localStorage.removeItem(KEY);localStorage.removeItem(OLD);localStorage.removeItem(WKEY)}catch(e){}
      toast('App lock is on. Sapp asks for the PIN when you come back to it.');render()
    }).catch(function(e){cryptoKey=null;var mm=$('pmsg');if(mm){mm.style.color='var(--r)';mm.textContent='Could not turn on the lock ('+((e&&e.message)||'error')+').'}})},
  lockOff:function(){
    var pn=$('p1'),m=$('pmsg');
    if(!pn||!pn.value){if(m)m.textContent='Enter your PIN.';return}
    openRec(pn.value).then(function(){
      try{localStorage.setItem(KEY,JSON.stringify(S));if(wallData)localStorage.setItem(WKEY,wallData)}
      catch(e){if(m)m.textContent='Could not save. Free some space and try again.';return}
      try{localStorage.removeItem(LK)}catch(e){}
      return migratePics(null).then(function(){saltB=null;render()})
    }).catch(function(){if(m)m.textContent='Wrong PIN.'})},

document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
  if(e.target.id==='scrim'&&sheet&&sheet!=='name'){A.closesheet();return}
  var b=e.target.closest('[data-a]');if(!b)return;
  var a=b.dataset.a;
  if(a!=='delsub'&&a!=='delnote'&&a!=='delproj')pend=null;
  if(!A[a])return;
  if(!allowed(a,b)){toast('Subscribe to Pro to use this.');return}
  A[a](b)});
document.addEventListener('input',function(e){
  var t=e.target;
  if(t.classList&&t.classList.contains('dmy'))maskDmy(t);
  if(t.getAttribute&&t.getAttribute('autocapitalize')==='words'&&t.type==='text'&&!t.classList.contains('dmy')){
    var cp=t.selectionStart,cv2=t.value.replace(/(^|[\s\-(\/])([a-z])/g,function(m,a,b){return a+b.toUpperCase()});
    if(cv2!==t.value){t.value=cv2;try{t.setSelectionRange(cp,cp)}catch(x){}}}
  if(t.id==='pq'){pq=t.value;clearTimeout(pqT);pqT=setTimeout(runSearch,110);return}
  if(t.id==='sylt'){sylTxt=t.value;var sp=$('sylp');if(sp)sp.textContent=sylPrev();return}
  if(t.dataset.cv){cvSet(t);return}
  if(t.dataset.q){ginput(t);return}

  else if(t.id==='qn'){S.scratch=t.value;save()}
  else if(t.id==='tg'){S.target=+t.value;$('tgv').textContent=t.value+'%';save()}});

  if(e.target.id==='pvp'){var dd=pdfs.find(function(x){return x.id===pvw.id}),nn2=parseInt(e.target.value,10);if(dd&&nn2>=1&&nn2<=dd.pages)pvw.page=nn2;rs();return}

  if(e.target.dataset.g){if(e.target.dataset.ev!==undefined)render()}

  else if(e.target.id==='sel'){S.sel=e.target.value;save();render()}
  else if(e.target.id==='tg')render();
  else if(e.target.id==='wf')A.upload(e.target);
  else if(e.target.id==='pa')A.padd(e.target)});

  if(t&&t.closest&&t.closest('.sheet')&&/INPUT|TEXTAREA|SELECT/.test(t.tagName))setTimeout(function(){try{t.scrollIntoView({block:'center',behavior:'smooth'})}catch(x){}},320)});

  if(e.key!=='Enter')return;
  var t=e.target;
  if(t.id&&t.id.indexOf('pf-')===0){
    e.preventDefault();
    var ins=[].slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('.sheet input[id^="pf-"]')),ix=ins.indexOf(t);
    if(ix>=0&&ix<ins.length-1)ins[ix+1].focus();else A.login();
    return}
  if(t.id==='pin')A.unlock();
  else if(t.id==='tk')A.addtask();
  else if(t.id==='wn')A.savename();
  else if(t.id==='et')A.addev();
  else if(t.id==='pn')A.addproj();
  else if(t.id==='nm')A.addsub();
  else if(t.id==='nt')A.addnote();
  else if(t.classList&&t.classList.contains('sin'))A.addsn({dataset:{id:t.dataset.id,c:t.dataset.c}})});

if(!locked){S.events=S.events.filter(function(e){return e.date>=ymd(new Date())});S.tasks=S.tasks.filter(function(t){return !t.done||t.date>=ymd(new Date())});migrateOldWall();restoreTimer();if(!S.name||!S.profileDone){sheet='name';sa=true}}

window.onRestore=function(j){
  if(!j){toast('That file is not a Sapp data file.');return}
  try{var o=JSON.parse(j);localStorage.clear();Object.keys(o).forEach(function(k){localStorage.setItem(k,o[k])});location.reload()}catch(e){toast('Could not read that data file.')}};
window.onBack=function(){
  if(sheet&&sheet!=='name'){A.closesheet();return true}
  if(tab!=='home'&&pro()){tab='home';an=true;render();return true}
  return false};
checkRem();setInterval(checkRem,30000);syncNative();
